1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/sa rba ra ha so ya na ma tan tra rA dzA/bod skad du
1b2(rgyud): thems cad gsang ba zhes/bya ba'i brgyud kyi rgyal po//bcom ldan 'das ye shes
1b3(rgyud): sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo//'byung lnga nyid kyis lnga'i bdag nyid can/
1b4(rgyud): mi rnams lus la nges par/mthong ba dag/rang sems rab tu sgoms par
2a1(rgyud): @##//gyur na ni der sgoms pas nges par sangs rgyas 'gyur/
2a2(rgyud): rang shes gsang ba dam pa ni/sangs rgyas ye shes bla med de//gnyis med
2a3(rgyud): gnyis kyi tshul can te//sangs rgyas rtag par rab tu 'dud//gang yang 'phra mo'i dngos
2a4(rgyud): po ci bzhig mthong//stug po'i dngos por de bzhin te//dngos dang dngos med khyad
2b1(rgyud): par ni;;bsgoms pa kun tu brtags pa yin//dngos po bsga ma pa dang a yin//bsgom par
2b2(rgyud): bya ba bsgom pa min//dngos po bsgoms par gyur pa yis//rang gi sems ni yongs su basbyong//sangs
2b3(rgyud): rgyas sangs rgyas yul ma yin//sems can yul du 'ang mi 'gyur gyi//rang sems rtogs pas sangs
2b4(rgyud): rgyas te;;sangs rgyas sangs rgyas brtogs pas min//gnyis med sbyor ba 'di la yang//ting nge 'dzin gi
2b5(rgyud): ye shes brtag/brgyu'i de nyid ci ltar mthong//'bras bu'i de nyid de nyid yin//rgyu'i de nyid bcung zad med//
3a1(rgyud): @##//'bras bu de nyid de bzhin no//de nyid de ni de bzhin gshegs//de la rnal 'byor pa kun spyod//rgyu yis
3a2(rgyud): rgyu ni dngos por min//'bras bu'i dngos por 'bras bus min//rgyu de 'bras bu'i dngos por rung//rang gi sems ni
3a3(rgyud): so sor snang//ye shes de ni rgyu dngos de//gsang de 'bras bu'i dngos po yin//gsang ba gnyis med sbyor ba
3a4(rgyud): yis||de'i dngos pos mchog yin no//de nyid de nyid rtogs gyur pa//gsang ste gsang ba'i rnal 'byor pa//gsang ba rig
3a5(rgyud): shes pa ste|mchog ni mchog gi rnal 'byor te//sdug pa'i ngo bor gang bshad pa//mi sdug pa 'ang de'i dngos//bsdug dang
3b1(rgyud): mi sdug gzugs gang yin//mi mthong de ni de bzhin gshegs//nam shes tsam ni med pa ru//rnal 'byor pa
3b2(rgyud): ni 'byung mi 'gyur//ci ltar ye shes mchog dag pa//de ltar dag pa de bzhin gshegs//rnam par shes
3b3(rgyud): pa rnam pa bzhis//phyag rgya bzhi'i cho ga yis//gsang ba gnyis med sbyor ba yis//sku gsung thugs ldan yang
3b4(rgyud): dag byung /thugs kyi phyag rgya chen po ste//dam tshig phyag rgya kun du 'gro//chos kyi phyag rgya gsung gi ste//las
3b5(rgyud): kyi phyag rgya sku yi yin//rgya yi rgyas btab dngos po ni//gsang bdag gsang ba' rnal 'byor pa'o//
4a1(rgyud): @##//rang rig pa'i ngo bo yis//ting 'dzin ye shes rtags pa yin//lus dag semasu rnam rtags pa'i//
4a2(rgyud): bya ba brgyud du rnam rtags pa//gsang ba rnam pa gsum du gshad//rdo rje las can sna tshogs bzhin//sangs rgyas lnga'i
4a3(rgyud): bdag nyid ci ni//gsang ba'i ye shes bla med yin//phyag rgya bzhi po gsang ba ste//rnal 'byor pa'i las 'grub byed
4a4(rgyud): 'byung ba chen po bzhi po yang//phyag rgya bzhi zhes bshad pa yin//phyag rgya bzhi'i mnyam sbyor ba//lha mo bzhi dang yang dag
4a5(rgyud): ldan;;sa ni spyan zhes bya ba yin//chu ni ma ma ki zhes bshad//me dang rlung zhes drags pa ni//gos kar mo dang sgrol ma yin//
4a6(rgyud): chu'i khams ni chu dkyil 'khor//dbang chen sa'i khams yin te//me ni me'i khams yin te//rlung ni rlung gi dkyil 'khor ro//
4b1(rgyud): dbang chen kha dog ser po ste//gru bzhi pa ste//brgyas pa yin//chu'i dkyil 'khor bzlum pa ste//dkar po'i 'dog can zhi ba
4b2(rgyud): yin;;me'i dkyil 'khor kha dog dmar//gru gsum dbang gi las la'o//rlung gi dkyil 'khor gzhu'i dbyibs//nag po gdug pa'i
4b3(rgyud): las rnams la//'byung ba che bdag las grub pa//las kyi dngos grub yon tan bzhi//las kyi dngos grub dngos grub mchog/
4b4(rgyud): 'khor lo sgyur ba rnam pa bzhi//zhi ba la ni spyan ga tshul//rgyas pa pad ma'i rdo rje can//dbang la rnam par snang mdzad gnas//
4b5(rgyud): rdo rje khro bo mngon spyod la'o//zhi ba dgongs ka bya ba yin//de bzhin rgyas pa snga dro la//gdug pa'i las rnams nyi ma'i gung//dbang gi las
4b6(rgyud): ni mtshan phyed bya//zhi ba la dkar pos sbyar bar bya//ser po snang ba rgyas pa la//dbang gi las la dmar po ste//gdug pa nag por rab tu
4b7(rgyud): sbyar;ting 'dzin ye shes las byung ba//las kyi sgrub pa dam pa yin//bcom ldan dag pa sgom bya ste//sangs rgyas gyur las mi g.yo
5a1(rgyud): @##//gyur//b+ha ga'i dbus su gsang ba'i bdag/rdo rje dbyings ni de bzhin gshegs//lnga'i bdag gyur bdag nyid ni//sgoms na g.yo
5a2(rgyud): ba med par 'gyur//snying la gnas pa'i lha mo che//rnal 'byor ma'i rnal 'byor skyed//sangs rgyas thams cad skyed mdzad yum//rdo rje dbyings
5a3(rgyud): kyi dbang mor bshad//'dod chags zhes sdang gti mug ste//'di dag 'jig rten dug gsum ste//sangs rgyas bcom ldan dug mi mnga'//
5a4(rgyud): rdo rje dbyings ni de bzhin bshegs//chags /dang sdang dang rmongs pa rnaMs//sgoms na 'khaMs/gsum pa'i bdag/sangs rgyas bcom ldan
5a5(rgyud): 'das gyur te//sangs rgyas bden pas dug bcom mo//rang sems so sor rtogs pa yis/gti mug sems ni de bzhin gshegs//
5a6(rgyud): rmongs pa nyid ni 'jig rten 'gyur//sems can kun la lta bar bya//zhes sdang sems ni de bzhin gshegs //phra mo'i ye shes yang dag skyed//sdang
5a7(rgyud): gyur rdo rje nyid du gyur//sems can thams cad gzhig par bya//'dod chags sems ni de bzhin gshegs//byang chub sems ni yang dag skyed//chags gyur
5a8(rgyud): bzang po nyid du gyur//sangs rgyas thaMs cad chags par bya//byams pa'i sems ni de bzhin gshegasang rgyal yang dag skyed nas ni//mdza' bor gyur
5b1(rgyud): cing kun nyid gyur//sangs rgyas kun nyid 'byung bar gyur//sangs rgyas kun la mkhyud par bya//chags pa'i lta ba byams pa ste//thugs rje chos ni smra ba dang//kun la mi 'jigs
5b2(rgyud): sbyin pa dang//sangs rgyas thaMs cad mnyes bya'o//rnal 'byor sbyin las rab dga' ste//tshul khrims ldan pas dri med 'gyur//bzod pa las ni 'od byed pa//brtson las 'od
5b3(rgyud): 'phro can dang ldan//bsam rtan las ni sbyang dka' ba//shes rab kyis ni mngon gyur mthong//thabs chen las ni ring du song//stobs dang ldan pas mi g.yo 'gyur//
5b4(rgyud): smon lam mis ni le lo can//ye shes ldan la chos kyi sprin//rgyal ba de bzhin gshegs pa ni//rang gi bdag nyid bcu gcig gyur//pha rol phyin
5b5(rgyud): pa bcu rnams dang//stobs dang dbang ni rdzogs gyur cing//sa bcu las ni 'das gyur pa//mtshan ma rtan gyur de las ni//rdo rje mchu ni rdzogs sangs
5b6(rgyud): rgyas//chos kyi bdud rtsi'i bcud len yin//sangs rgyas kun kyi mtha' yas pa//bzung dang 'dzin pa spangs pa yin//chos kyi bdud brtsi bcud kyis len//rdo rje'i
5b7(rgyud): 'chu ni dag mthungs pas//rdo rje slob gyur rnal 'byor pa//gdod nas dag par de tshe 'gyur//gdod nas dag par gnas gyur pa//de nas byang chub sems su 'gyur//
5b8(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//rnam par grol ba'i ris kyis g.yogs//byang chub sems ni bla na med//yang dag skyed 'gyur rnal 'byor pa//rdo rje seMs
6a1(rgyud): @#//dpa' snying la 'dug/sems can khams ni mtha' yas pa'i//mar gyur dam tshig 'dzin byed dam//rdo rje dam gis spyad na//rtag tu dam tshig gnas par
6a2(rgyud): 'gyur;;khams gsum lam ni nyams dga' bar//bud med ces bshad ji snyed pa//cho ga bzhin du kun spyad cing//thugs kyi rdo rje smad mi bya//rdo rje gsum gi bsku la gnas//
6a3(rgyud): dam tshig phyag rgya ji snyed pa//rdo rje dam tshig gsum gis bya//gsung gi rdo rje smad mi bya//rdo rje sems dpa' las byung ba'i//phyag rgya kun kyi bud med bzang//de rnams sten pa'i
6a4(rgyud): rnal 'byor pa//phyag rgya thams cad dbang du 'gyur//'jig rten khams ni thos cad na//bud med ces bya ji snyed pa//phyag rgya chen po'i sbyor ba yis//de dag kun la
6a5(rgyud): nye bar spyad//gsang ba mchog gi rnal 'byor pa//ting 'dzin ye shes dam pa yin//gal te dngos grub mchog 'dod na//nga rgyal ma yin hUM ma yin//rig pa ma yin sngags
6a6(rgyud): rig min;;lag pa'i phyag rgya 'ang mi bca'o//mchod rten gi ni las mi bya//blegs bam dag kyang blag mi bya//dkyil 'khor dag kyang mi bya zhing//rdo rje mchog gsum phyag mi 'tshal//
6a7(rgyud): slob dpon smad par mi bya ste//bla ma sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam//khyod kyis srog chags gsad par bya//rdzun kyi tshig kyang smra bar bya//khyed kyis ma byin blang mi bya zhing//gzhan
6a8(rgyud): gyi bud med sten par bya//sbyin sreg yang na bsam rten ni//lus ngag yid ni mi byed pa//gzhan min sems kyis byas pa ste//'di ni bsod pa'i mchog yin no//sngags rnams
6b1(rgyud): thams cad rab tu grub//rnal 'byor kun kyang rnam kun tu//de bas 'bad pa thams cad kyis//sangs rgyas chos nyid sgom par bya//ma 'dres chos ni bco rgyad po//sangs rgyas
6b2(rgyud): chos zhes bshad pa yin//de rnams rtag tu sgoms pa ni//sangs rgyas chos ni sgrub byed yin//bsam rtan bzhi dang gzugs med bzhi//rtag tu thob par byas pa dang//'gog pa'i
6b3(rgyud): snyoms 'jug de yis ni//sangs rgyas byang chub rab tu sgrub//byang chub phyogs ni sum bcu bdun//chos su yang dag bshad pa gang//de rnaMs sgoms pas dag pa dang//
6b4(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' 'grogs phan yin//'gro ba rnaMs la sros pa'i sems//brtan bzhi'i snying po rab sgom pa//stong pa'i de nyid las byung ba//rdo rje ye shes zhes
6b5(rgyud): byar bshad//chos bdag med las yang dag byung//gnyis med ye shes dam pa de//chos nyid mchog gi ye shes ni//chos dbyings ye shes yang dag bshad//'di ni
6b6(rgyud): chos dbyings ye shes te//rdo rje lta bu'i ting 'dzin skyes//rdo rje ye shes rang bzhin sems//rdo rje dbyings zhes bya bar bshad//sems kyi spro ba'i sbyor ba yis//phra mo'i ye shes swogs
6b7(rgyud): sbyor ba//de bzhin gshegs kun ye shes ni//rnam par snang mdzad zhes byar bshad//byams pa spro ba'i sbyor ba yis//spyod pa spyod par byed pa'i mchog/zag med rtan par 'gyur ba'i//
6b8(rgyud): thugs ni mi skyod pa zhes bshad//thugs rje spro ba'i tshul gis ni//sems can pha thogs mdzad pa'o//thugs ni 'gro don yang dag ldan//rin chen 'byung ldan zhes bshad do//
7a1(rgyud): @#//dga' bas spro ba'i sbyor ba yis//theg chen mchog ni dri ma med//thugs ni 'od gsal dag pa ni//'od dpag med ces de bshad do//btang snyoms spro bar mdzad pa'i
7a2(rgyud): thugs :sems can thams cad brtogs mdzad pa//don yod bla med bya ba ni//don yod grub par de bshad do//byang chub rang bzhin ye shes mchog/de 'dir rdo rje zhes bshad de//rdo rje
7a3(rgyud): ye shes las byung ba//rdo rje sems dpar de bshad do//sangs rgyas thams cad spyan 'dren pa//rdo rje lcags skyu zhes bshad te//rdo rje'i rigs rnams kun kyi rgyal//rdo rje rgyal por
7a4(rgyud): de bshad do//sangs rgyas mthas cad tshogs byed pas//mnyes par byed pa zhes bshad de//bdud kyi sbyor ba chags pa che//rdo rje chags par de bshad do//sangs
7a5(rgyud): rgyas thams cad mnyes byed cing//legs so zhes pa rab rtsol ba//byang chub sems dpa' dga' byed pas//rdo rje legs par de bshad do//byang cub sems dpa'i sbyin pa gang//
7a6(rgyud): nam mkha' rdo rje las byung ba//don yod rin chen chen po de//rdo rje rin chen de bshad do//ma rig bral ba'i ye shes ni//tshogs rnams la ni nges par 'byung//thar pa'i snang ba gzi rjid
7a7(rgyud): che//rdo rje nyi mar de bshad do//rnam pa kun kyi dngos ye shes//thar pa'i lam 'jug thob mdzad gang//chos kyi rgyal mtshan tog chen po//rdo rje dpal du de bshad do//rdo rje zhad pa rnal
7a8(rgyud): 'byor che//sangs rgyas kun 'dzum rmad 'byung ba//'di ni byang chub sems dpa' che//rdo rje gzhad par de bshad do//sangs rgyas kun kyi rnal 'byor che//sangs rgyas chos ni rtogs mdzad pa//
7b1(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos dag pa//rdo rje chos su de bshad do//shes rab rnon por theg pa che//nyon mongs thams cad nges spyod mdzad//shes bya'i sgrib pa spyod pa'o//rdo rje rnon por de
7b2(rgyud): bshad do;;sangs rgyas ral mo 'khor lo ni//chos kyi 'khor lo rab skor ba//sems dpa' chen po theg pa che//rdo rje rgyu zhes de bshad do//shes rab ye shes rang bzhin dag/gsang
7b3(rgyud): gi sgra ni rnam par spangs//'jam pa'i gsung gi bdag nyid 'gyur//rdo rje smra bar de bshad do//pha rol tu phyin pa kun kyi zhabs//chos rnams thams cad brtogs mdzad pa//sems
7b4(rgyud): can kun don sprul pa po//rdo rje las zhes bya bar bshad;;sangs rgyas rjes dran yang dag grub//theg pa dman;pa rnam spangs shing//byang chub sems la rab zhugs pa//
7b5(rgyud): rdo rje srung bar de bshad do//sdig pa thaMs cad zad byed cing//de bzhin nyon mongs rnam sreg la//nyon mongs zad pa nyer len p+sa//rdo rje'i gnod sbyin de bshad do//sku
7b6(rgyud): gsung thugs kyi rdo rje rnams//bcing ba ru ni yang dag gyur//de nyid de nyid ye shes te//rdo rje khu zur zhes bshad do//dam tshig phyag rgya thams cad ni//rdo rje sdams las yang dag 'byung//
7b7(rgyud): sbyin de bshad do//rdo rje khu tshur gnyis gyur pa//las kyi phyag rgya zhes bshad do//nga rgyal med cing snang ba med//mtshan ma med cing bzhen pa med//bdag med mnyam nyid ye shes ni;;
7b8(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' zhes byar bshad//gsang ba'i mnyam par sbyor ba 'dir//ye shes pha rol phyin dge ba//rnam pa bcu gnyis yang dag 'byung//rdo rje pha rol phyin pa'i 'od//byang chub
8a1(rgyud): @#//sems ni rab sgom pas//pha rol sangs rgyas nyid son pas//pha rol phyin pa thob shes ni//rdo rje sems dmar bshad pa yin//bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhi rnams ni//
8a2(rgyud): shes rab pha rol phyin rdo rje//chos bzhi gcig tu gyur pa ni//rdo rje rin chen mar bshad do//gsang ba'i ye she ye shes che//de nyid pha rol phyin dge ba//pha rol phyin pa che las byung//rdo rje chos mar
8a3(rgyud): shes par bya//pha rol phyin par dge ba ni//sbyor ba'i thibasu rab rtags pa//rdo rje las mar shes par bya//gang phyir de ni rol par dge/skye mo sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin//tshul khrims pha rol phyin
8a4(rgyud): dge bas||rgyan pa'i yan lag yid 'ong yin//bklu ma bzod pa'i pha rol phyin//ched du brjod pa'i chos rjod pa'o//gar ma brtson 'grus pha rol tu phyin//myur du sangs rgyas
8a5(rgyud): nyid rten pa'o//bdug pa shes rab pha rol phyin//kun tu khyab pa spos phyir ro//me tog bsam rtan pha rol phyin//'dod pa'i 'dam gis ma gos pa'o//mar me smon lam pha rol phyin
8a6(rgyud): phyogs kun snang bar byed pa yin//dri ni thabs kyi pha rol phyin//dri ngan mthur sel dge ba yis//sangs rgyas kun kyi thabs chen po//dbye ba rnam pa bzhi'i gnas//thams cad 'dod pa'i
8a7(rgyud): thabs chen pa//rdo rje lcags skya zhes bshad do//byang chub spyad pa spyod byed pa//rdo rje zhags pa zhes bshad do//theg pa 'di la rten 'gyur gang//rdo rje lcags sgrog zhes bshad do//chos rnams
8a8(rgyud): thams cad ma skyes shing//gnod nas zha ba mkha' dang mnyam//de lta'i tshig 'di rib 'jug pas//rdo rje dril bu zhes bshad do//tshogs gnyis ni sbyor ba yis//sems can kun kyi don
8b1(rgyud): ston pa'i :sems ni rang bzhin gis 'od gsal//bzla ba'i dkyil 'khor yin par bshad//yang dag rdo rje rtogs pa ste//'dzin pa'i sbyor nyid lcags skyu yin//phra bar 'bigs
8b2(rgyud): pas mda' zhes bya//dgyes par legaso zhes bya ba//sbas pas rin po che ru bshad/gzi rjid ces bya nyi ma yin//sgreng bas tog tu bshad pa ste//rnam par 'byed pas bshad par bshad//
8b3(rgyud): 'dod chags dag pa pad ma yin//nyon mongs dag gcod ral dri ste//dkyil 'khor sbyor bas 'khor lo yin//ngag gi spyod pas smra bar bshad//kun nyid sna tshogs rdo rje
8b4(rgyud): yin;;gzhi ka'i sbyor bas go cha yin//'jigs pa'i sbyor bas (bsar che ba ste//phyag rgya sbyor nyid 'ching ba yin//dkyil 'khor du ni gang bshad pa//yid kyi dkyil 'khor dam pa
8b5(rgyud): gzhal med khang gi ye shes te//sems kyi rtsegs pa bsgreng ba yin//phyi'i gru bzhi ba gang yin//tshad gis mnyam pa nyid gyur pa//byams pa laswogs sems gang yin//
8b6(rgyud): thig bzhi zhes ni bshad pa yin//chos kyi rjes dran sbyor ba nyid//gang de rdo rje thig tu bshad//lta ba kun las rnam grol ba//ye shes thig tu bshad pa yin//tshul khrims thos kyi
8b7(rgyud): dam pa ni;;gang gi rgyan du bshad pa yin//dad dbang laswogs sems gang yin//ra ba lngar ni yang dag byung//rnam pa thar pa bzhi po ni//sgo rnamasu ni yang dag bshad//yang dag
8b8(rgyud): spong ba bzhi po ni//rta babs rnams dang bka' bar byung//dran pa nyer gzhag bzhi po ni//khams rnams bzhi por shes par bya//rdzu 'phrul rkang pa bzhi po ni//de ni sgo khyud rnam bzhi yin//
9a1(rgyud): @#//byang chub yan lag bdun pa ni//'phreng ba me tog chun rgyan pa//'phags lam yan lag rgyad pa ni//bka' ba brgyad ces bya bar bshad//sgrib pa gnyis las rnam
9a2(rgyud): zhing ;ting 'dzin ye shes gyis rtags pa//lam gi ye shes khyad par rnams//lnga yab ces ni yang dag bshad//nyon mongs ces ni nges gcod cing//nyon mongs 'phrog pas dra ba ste//
9a3(rgyud): yang dag gcod cing spong ba nyid//sel ba'i spong ba la gnas pa//tshogs gnyis kyi ni sbyor ba nyid//nyon mongs pa'i phyed 'phrog pa//phra ba phyed ces bya bar bshad//rang bzhin 'od
9a4(rgyud): gsal gang yin te//byang chub sems ni bla med yin//sems ni don kun skyed byed pa//bzla ba'i dkyil 'khor yin par bshad//shes rab ye shes rang bzhin dag/rdo rje
9a5(rgyud): lta bu'i ting 'dzin skyes//thar pa'i snang ba gzi rjid che//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor yin par bshad//mtshon rtsi lngar ni yang dag bshad//lta ba lnga yis me tog yin//ye shes
9a6(rgyud): lnga yis nges sbyor nyid//sangs rgyas lnga zhes yang dag bshad//namakha' bzhin du bdag med pa'i//rnam shes ye shes dam pa yin//ye shes de'i rgyan bshad pa//chags pa'i seMs
9a7(rgyud): zhes bya bar bshad//chags bral las ni byang chub 'khyud//chags pa'i sems las gzhan yin no//de bas chags dang chags bral ba//byang chub sems zhes yang dag bshad//
9a8(rgyud): 'jig rten chags dang bral bas btul//phyag rgya'i dngos brub chags las byung//de ni thabs kyi sgoms pa dag/dngos grub thams cad ster ba yin//ji ltar 'jig rten 'dul 'gyur ba//
9b1(rgyud): de ltar rnal 'byor pa yis bshad//ci ltar dul bya dag gyur pa//zhen pa kun tu chod par 'gyur//mtshan nyid gang zhig yang dag bshad//mtshan nyid des ni mtshon par 'gyur//sku gsung
9b2(rgyud): thugs kyi phyag rgya yis//rgyas btab ye shes yin par bshad//phyag rgya sgoms pas rnal 'byor pa//rtag tu phyag rgya'i bdag can gyur//rgyas btab dngos po shes nas ni//sgoMs nas
9b3(rgyud): phyag rgyas btab par 'gyur//mtshan dang mtshan nyid 'brel ba yin//shes nas rnal 'byor yang dag spyod//ci ltar mtshan nyid yang dag grub//de bzhin dag pa myur du 'grub//g.yas
9b4(rgyud): pa'i cha ni mtshan ma ni//shes rab can ni rab tu rtan//g.yon kyi mtshan ma ci ltar mthong//de'i thugs rje mtshon pa yi//phyag ni gnyis ka mnyam sbyor bas//
9b5(rgyud): ting 'dzin gnyisu bshad pa yin//zhabs gnyis kyis ni mtshan ma yis//tshogs gnyis dag ni rten pa yin//sor mo lnga'i mnyam sbyor bas//dkyil 'khor lnga yis
9b6(rgyud): rgyan pa yin//sangs rgyas lnga yis mngon byang chub//rim pa bzhin du sten bya ste//gang zhig gung mo ston pa ni//namakha' las ni byung ba mtshon//gang zhig 'dzub mo bston pa ni//des ni
9b7(rgyud): dbang chen dkyil 'khor yin//sprin lag tu ni yang dag bshad//chu'i dkyil 'khor zhes bshad do//gang zhig mthe bong ston pa ni//de'i yis me ni mtshon pa yin//gang zhig mthe' chung
9b8(rgyud): ston pa ni//rlung gi dkyil 'khor nyid du 'gyur//gang zhig lte ba ston pa ni//rdo rje dbyings gi dbang phyug bshad//gang zhig snying kha ston pa ni//rdo rje sems dpa'ar rjesu dag/gang zhig smin
10a1(rgyud): @#//'tshams ston pa ni//rdo rje rin chen ma zhes bya//gang zhig lkog ma ston pa ni//rdo rje chos mar shes par bya//gang zhig spyi bo ston pa ni//rdo rje las mar shes par bya//
10a2(rgyud): byang chub lngar mngon byang chub bam//yang na ye shes lnga rnams sam//sangs rgyas lnga dang yang dag ldan//dkyil 'khor byang chub yin par stan//sngon kyi sgra ni ji ltar bshad//mdun
10a3(rgyud): du de ni rab tu stan//rgyab gi sgra ni ci ltar bshad//phyi mar de ni yang dag stan//gang du thog ma'i sgrar gyur pa//der ni rgyu'i sgra ru stan//gang zhig mtha' ma'i sgrar gyur pa//'dir ni 'bras
10a4(rgyud): bur yang dag stan//sangs rgyas bcom ldan gnyis mi mnga'//rgyu dang 'bras bur ldan gyur min//gang zhig thig ma mtha' ma med//de phyir rtag pa zhes bshad do//
10a5(rgyud): rang sems rtogs pas sangs rgyas te//rtogs par bya ba 'ang seMs nyid yin//sangs rgyas zhing zhes bshad pa dag/byang chub semasu bshad par bya//rang bzhin dag pa'i
10a6(rgyud): seMs can ni//kun rig par yang dag bshad//seMs can rang bzhin dag pa yin//byang chub sems su shes par bya//dang por gyur pa snying kar 'debs//brtan pa nyid du 'ang rang nyid 'gyur//
10a7(rgyud): skra 'dzin pa ni ril pa'i dbus//'od dpag med du gnyam gzhag yin//btsum pa nar ni rjes bshad pa//de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje gsung//dbang po gnyis ni mnyam sbyor ba//rnam par snang
10a8(rgyud): mdzad ces bshad do//bu mo lo grangs bcu gnyis par//skyes mchog thub pa'i gang gsungs pa//pha rol phyin pa bcu gnyis su//gsung gis gzhan du mi rtag go/bu mo lo grangs bcu drug bar//
10b1(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pas gang gsungs pa//rdo rje sems bswogs seMs dpa' yi//sgom pa'i don gi sgra yin no//lha mo lo grangs nyi shu bar//'khor sgyur bas 'di skad gsungs//mchod pa nyi shu'i
10b2(rgyud): bdag nyid can//nyi shu dang po rims bzhin no//rang gi chung ma rang rig las//'byung bas rang rig yin par bshad//gzhan gi chung mar gang stan pa//gzhan bsdug gzhan gi rig pa yin//rtsi dang
10b3(rgyud): rdo rje zhes bshad pa//rdo rje ye shes bla med yin//bshang dang pad ma zhes gshad pa//chos kyi ye shes bla med yin//yongs su gsal bar rnams shes pa//dkyil 'khor thugs kyi dam
10b4(rgyud): pa yin;;sku gsung thugs kyi spyod pa yis//'bad pa yis ni mchod byed pa//rang gi rdo rje pad mar ldan//dbang po gnyis las byung ba yi//rang gi khu ba thig le yis//
10b5(rgyud): rdo rje seMs dpa' mchod par bya//tshogs gnyis kyi ni sbyor ba yis//sangs rgyas thaMs cad gcig sdus pa//gnyis med gnyis nyid la rten pa//thugs rje shugs dang yang dag ldan//dbang
10b6(rgyud): po gnyis kyi nyams sbyor bas//rang gi khu ba mkhas pa yis//rnal 'byor chen po phyug nas ni//dkyil 'khor yang dag byin gyis rlabs//dgod dam rab tu smra ba 'am//sangs rgyas rol mo'i gar byed pa//
10b7(rgyud): lha mo bzhi'i mnyam sbyor bar//rgyu dang mi rgyu rnaMs rol//gsar zhing legs par sgril ba dang//tshad dang ldan zhing mdzes pa yis//shes rab can kyi thig btab pa//sku gsung thugs kyi ni sgoMs pas so//
10b8(rgyud): zur bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//brta babs bzhis ni mdzes byas la//thig bzhi dang ni yang dag ldan//dar dang me tog 'phreng bas rgyan//bgrwa'i 'tshams ni thams cad dang//sgo khyud kyi ni mtshams rnamasu//
11a1(rgyud): @#//rdo rje rin chen spras par ni//phyi'i dkyil 'khor thig le gdab po//rdo rje thig gi(sa) yongs skor cing//bka' ba brgyad kyis nye bar mdzes//bka' ba brgyad kyi nang gnas pa'i//
11a2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor rgyad par bstan pa dang//grwa'i dkyil 'khor dor bar ni//rdo rje lta ma tshogs kyis mchod//'khor lo lnga dang yang dag ldan//dkyil 'khor lnga yis rnam par rgyan//dra ba dra phyed kyis
11a3(rgyud): spras shing//brnga yab kyis ni nyer spras pa//'khru ni bcu gnyis bya ba 'am//'khru bcu drug tu rab tu rtag/yang na khru ni nyi shu tsam//nyi shu lnga dang ldan pa yis//'khru ni nyi shu lnga pa yi//
11a4(rgyud): dkyil 'khor yang dag byin gyis rlab//sgo rnams kyi ni dbus ma'i cha//cha dgu ru ni rnam dbye' ba//dkyil 'khor sgo ru bses nas ni//sgo khyud der ni bya ba yin//sgo khyud dkyil
11a5(rgyud): 'khor rnam pa ni//snying po'i dkyil de 'khor dang mnyam par//dgu bcA ru ni rnam dgos te//dgra'i dkyil 'khor dor bar bya//tha mar khyams dang ldan pa yi//dkyil 'khor lngas ni//
11a6(rgyud): ldan pa dang//kha dog lnga dang yang dag ldan/dgra bzhi par ni rtag par bya//lta ba lnga po grod dga' che//kha dog lnga'i de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas lnga dang mnyam sbyor bas//sa ni kun
11a7(rgyud): mtshon gyis rab tu sgyur//dkyil 'khor lnga'i dbus su ni//sangs rgyas lnga ni dgod par bya//kha dog lnga dang ting 'dzin gyis//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga yis khyab//shel dang bzla ba'i ('o)'dra zhing ('a)tud
11a8(rgyud): sangs rgyas thaMs cad gcig sdu ba//de bzhin gshegs pa rnam snang mdzad//dpa' bo'i lta ba de bzhin gshegs//in 'dra na le 'od 'dra zhing//rdo rje thaMs cad gcig sdus pa//mi skyod yi
11b1(rgyud): shes las byung ba//rdo rje'i ltas pa de bzhin gshegs//'dzam bu'i gser gi 'od 'dra zhing//sangs rgyas thas cad gcig sdus pa//sems can kun kyi don du 'byung//'bar ba'i
11b2(rgyud): lta ba de bzhin gshegs//dpa'o chen po pad ma 'dra//pad ma ra ga'i nor bu'i 'od//chos kyi ye shes las byung ba//chags pa'i lta ba de bzhin gshegs/mar rgad kyi ni 'dog 'dra zhing//dang
11b3(rgyud): sangs rgyas thas cad gcig stus pa//sangs rgyas kun 'jig las byung ba//byams pa'i lta ba de bzhin gshogs//dbus su ma sa'i bzhi dag pa//nor bu chu shel 'dog 'dra ba//shar phyogs nyam
11b4(rgyud): tu sngon pa che//lho ru ser po yang dag ldan//nub phyor der ni dmar po ste//byang du ljang khu yang dag ldan//kun tu khas//dang ldan pa la//sgor ni khyams dang bral ba'o//gang zhig dbus
11b5(rgyud): mar yang dag bshad//sangs rgyas ye shes bla med yin//gzhan dag don kun yang dag ldan//ting 'dzin ye shes dam pa yin//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas pas//bzla ba'i
11b6(rgyud): dkyil 'khor dam pa bsam//sangs rgyas kun ni rnam sgoms la//spyan ni dbus su bsam par bya//bzhin bzangs spyan ni yangs pa dang//nor bu chu shel mdog 'dra la//mtshan rnams thaMs cad yongs
11b7(rgyud): su rdzogs//bud med sgyu ma'i mchog 'dzin la//phyag tu 'khor lo sgom bya ste//khams gsum pa ni dbang byed pa//dngos grub kun byed ye shes ni//'khor lo yid bzhin nor bu mdog/nam mkha' rdo
11b8(rgyud): rje dbus chud par//bzla ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa bsam //sangs rgyas sku ni rnam sgom la//nam mkha'i rdo rje mchog sgom mo//zhal mdzes spyan ni yangs pa dang //ud pal sngon po'i mdab ma'i 'dog/
12a1(rgyud): @#//mtshan rnams thaMs cad yang dag rdzogs//namakha'i sgyu ma'i mchog bsamos//phyag tu ud pal sngon po ste//khams gsuM pas ni phyag byas pa//sangs rgyas byang chub byed pa'i
12a2(rgyud): mchog;;gsang ba'i dngos grub rdo rje can//namakha' rdo rje dbus chud par//bzla ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa bsam//sangs rgyas sku ni rnam sgom la//rdo rje chos mar rnam par bsam//
12a3(rgyud): mdzes pa'i zhal ni spyan yangs pa//pad ma ra ga lta bu'i 'dog/sku 'dog dmar po yang dag 'byung//chags pa'i pad ma mdzin sdug pa//mtshan rnams thaMs cad yag dag rdzog/rgyan rnams
12a4(rgyud): kun kyis rnam par rgyan//phyag tu ud pal dmar po ni//sangs rgyas thaMs cad rab sgoms pa'o//chos kyi ye shes byed pa 'chog/gsang ba dam tshig rdo rje can//mkha' dbyings rdo rje
12a5(rgyud): dbus chud pa//bzla ba'i dkyil 'khor ngam pa sgom//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnams sgom la//sgrol ma mchog ni bsam par bya//zhal mdzes spyan ni yangs pa dang//sna tshogs
12a6(rgyud): rgyan kyis rnam par rgyan//kha dog ser po nyid bsam zhing//bud med sgyu ma'i dregs rdo ba//phyag tu ud pal dam pa ni//ser po snang ba bsam par bya//rdo rje nyid 'dzin las byung ba//sems can kun
12a7(rgyud): kyis phyag byas po//oM ru ru s+phu ru dza la ti sh+tha sid d+ha lo tsa ne//swa rba a r+tha swa da na swI hA/oM shang ka re shan ti ka re/g+hu Ta Tu g+hu Ta Ti ni g+hu ta ya g+hu Ta Ti ni swAhA//oM ka Te ba'i ka Te ka taM ka te swAhA//oM ta re tud
12a8(rgyud): ta re swAhA//rig pa'i lha mo bzhi'o//sku ni lus kyi ngo bo yis//thugs ni sems kyi rnam pa nyid//gsung ni ngag gi rnam pa yis//mchod na mchod pa'i rnam dag nyid//sku gsung thugs kyi gsang ba ni//
12b1(rgyud): mkhas pa yis ni srung bya ste//srungs na mchog tu gsang ba ni//rtag par kun tu 'byung bar 'gyur//dngos po yongs su dag pa ni//med par kun tu rtag mi 'byung//rnal 'byor pa'i rnal 'byor thob//
12b2(rgyud): tshems med yang dag mi mthong ngo//de bas gsang ba'i ye shes 'byung//rnal 'byor kun tu 'byung bar 'gyur //shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i thabs//ting 'dzin ye shes rtags pa yin//chos rnams thaMs cad
12b3(rgyud): rnam dag pa//dag pa nyid du gyur pa ni//ci ltar lha'i nor bu ni//rang gi 'od kyis rtag tu snang//srid pa yongasu dag pa yang//rnal 'byor rnal 'byor thob byed yin//rdo rje dbyings ni gsang chen du//
12b4(rgyud): gang zhig dbang skur mkhas pa la//thugs kyi gsang ba'i rnal 'byor mchog/mkhas pas de la bshad par bya//thaMs cad gsang ba zhes bya ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po brdzogas+ho////
12b5(rgyud): rgya gar kyi mkhan po pad ma ka ra bar ma dang//zhu chen gyi lo tsha ba dge slong rin chen bzang pos sgyur cing zhus te rtan la phab pa//!//!//
12b6(rgyud): @#///bcom ldan 'das 'jaM dpal gzho nur gyur pa la phyag 'phel lo////dpa'o gcig grub dpa' brtso//rab mchog smra ba rnaMs kyi mchog/'gro ba'i bla ma 'jaM dpal rdo rjes//
12b7(rgyud): 'jig rten du rgyud rab gsungs//lha nyid gsang sngags tshul gyis ni//dngos grub thaMs cad rtsol mdzad pa//sngags pas de nyid sgoms nas ni//de grub na ni 'grub 'gyur te//sngags pa lus
12b8(rgyud): ni rnam dag dang//gang tshe dngos grub phra mo 'grub//snod du gyur pa'i lus la ni//dngos grub chen po rab tu skye//rim pa 'di yis 'di la ni//sa laswogs pa 'thob pa skye//sngags pas yid
13a1(rgyud): @#//bzhin nor bu ltar//rtag tu sems can don byed do//de ltar mkhyen nas thugs rtses non//rdo rje 'chang ba swogs kyis zhus tshe//grub pa'i (ga)sgang sngags sdus pa ni//'gro ba'i
13a2(rgyud): don du gsungs pa yin//oM ka lu me lu (2)stam pa ya shi lA bar ShaM tu Shar bar ShaM tsa//lu pa i (2)swahA//gsang sngags kyi rgyal po 'di ni legs par bzlas nas ser ba gcod par byed do//
13a3(rgyud): dran pa nyid dang//thal ba la mngon par bzlas pas ser ba 'bab pa 'am//kha ba 'bab pa gcod par 'gyur//gang du 'dod pa der 'gro'o//de bzhin du glog dang//rlung dang/rdo rje dang//
13a4(rgyud): thog 'bab pa la sogs med par byed do//oM ga ru Tu hUM sa he//tsa la tsa la swahA//sngags kyi rgyal po 'di 'dra ba nyid na//ser ba 'bab pa zlog par byed do//rdo rgyus kyis rnga pa ta ha la bris
13a5(rgyud): shing;;rnga lcag la mngon par bzlas pa'i rnga pa ta ha'i sgras/ser ba 'bab pa bzlog par byed do//de bzhin du btud laswogs pa'i sgras kyang bya'o//oM he he/ti Sh+tha ti Sh+tha//
13a6(rgyud): b+han d+ha b+han d+ha/ha ra ya ha ra ya/ni run d+ha ni run haM/che ge mo ur N+Na ma ni swahA//gsang sngags kyi rgyal po 'di bsngon du snyen pa'i tshogs 'bum phrag cig yongasu zlas na 'grub par 'gyur ro//
13a7(rgyud): glog dang rlung dang/rdo rje dang/thog 'bab pa 'am/kha ba lasogs pa dran pa nyid kyis zlog par byed do//thal ba dang yungs kar la mngon par zlas na/char ches pa gcod pa dang/byi ba dang sgang ma
13a8(rgyud): lasogs pa'i 'chu 'ching bar byed do//skyed mos tsha la lam/zhing la bya dang 'bu dang srin dang/byi ba rnams kyis nye bar 'tshe ba bzlog par byed do//du khrod kyi ras la ming dang bcas pa'i sngags bris la/
13b1(rgyud): sra tshil gis btums te//dur khrod kyi bum par bcug nas kha bcad te//de nyid du sbas pas mdal rengs par 'gyuro//phyung nas 'o mas bkrus te//chu la pho bas zhi bar 'gyuro//
13b2(rgyud): yung ba 'am ba blas gro gas 'am//gos sam lo ma la mngon par bris te//spra tshil gi nad du btums pa'i sngags 'di//grog 'khar kyi sa la ji pa'i gzugasu byas pa de'i
13b3(rgyud): snying khar bcug te//de kha thal bas bkang nas sbas pas//dgol ba dang/phyir dgol ba'i kha rengs par byed do//rtsod sbyong byed pa na gzugs de nyid kyi lce la//lan bdun
13b4(rgyud): sngags pa'i tshar ma bdun gyis btsugs nas//kha 'ching ba byed par 'gyur ro//rdo rgyus kyi kham por la bris te//thal bas bkang la kha sbyar te//sbas na mi snyan pa
13b5(rgyud): rjod pa kha rengs par byed do//rdza mkhan kyi lag pa phyis pa'i bya bi las//lug gi gzugs su byas pa'i snying khar spra tshil gyis btums pa'i sngags bcug na/khros
13b6(rgyud): pa'i kha rengs par byed do////skye 'am dpung pa la btab na/g.yul dogs kyi mtshon cha dpal zhi bar 'gyuro//grog 'khar kyi 'byim pa las/byas pa'i//tshogs kyi bdag po'i rkang pa'i mthil
13b7(rgyud): du sngags kyi rgyal po 'di bcug ste//bzhi mdor sbas na 'gro ba dang//'ong ba thams cad rengs par byed cing//rtag par byed pa thams cad rengs par byed//gro ga 'am gos la sngags
13b8(rgyud): kyi rgyal po 'di bris la//spra tshil gis btums te de nyid kyi khog par bcug la/bum pa gsar pa chus bkang ba'i nang du bcug na/yongs su rtag pa rengs par byed do//bla gos kyi mtha' ma/
14a1(rgyud): @#//Ila sngags rjesu dran zhing 'dud pa bcings pas/lam du rkun ma laswogs rengs par byed cing//nags kyi ba men lasogs pa rwa can dang//mche ba can rnams rengs par byed
14a2(rgyud): do|bong ba la lan bdun sngags te/chur bcug na/chu na rgyu ba'i kha 'ching bar byed do//dur khrod kyi ras la pha rol gyi dpung gi tshogs kyi skyes bu'i brtso bo'i ming smos pa'i sngags sna tshogs rdo rje'i
14a3(rgyud): dbus su bris la/sna tshogs rdo rje'i phyi rol tu yi ge lam rgyad bris shing/phyi rol dbang chen kyi dkyil 'khor dang//ldan par bril te/phyogs rdo rjes rgyan pa'i spra chil las byas pa'i tshogs kyi
14a4(rgyud): bdag po'i khog par bcug pa'i sngags phyi rol gi dpung gi tshogs kyi/sa 'tshams kyi bar tu bcug na/dpung gi tshogs|/rengs par byed do//sngags oM gnyis ka sbyar du bcug la
14a5(rgyud): yi ge tha zung gis yongs su skor ba/yi ge lang rgyad kyis skor ba dbang chen kyi dkyil 'khorkyis mnon pa/sna tshogs rdo rje nye bar mdzes par byas pa/grog 'khar gi 'jami Ima II
14a6(rgyud): pa'i tshogs kyi bdag po'i khong par bcug la/dur khrod kyi bum par bcug nas/dur khrod du sbas pa 'am/pub ma pa'i sbyin sregs kyis pha rol kyi tshog+sa rengs par byed do//grong khyer tshig pa'i/e Shod B
14a7(rgyud): du phyogs shing/sngags 'dis mngon par sngags pa'i chu snyim pa bdun rab tu rto ra na/khyim gang srungs par byed pa'i khyim de srung bar byed do//thal bas 'gul pa bcings paIIIIIIIIIIIIIII
14a8(rgyud): byed do||mthe'u chung gi rtse mo dam du bcings la/sngags brjod pas skyigs bu med par byed do//thal ba la mngon par sngags pas/mig rengs pa sos par byed do//'brud+ma du zhi ba Iswogs (pa) pa rnam p+Ra
14b1(rgyud): 'tshe ba la ras kyi dum bu la bris te/khyim gang gi sgor btags pa'i khyim der 'brum bu laswogs pa rnam par 'tshe ba bzlog par byed do//nya dang/sha dang/chang dang/nas chung laswogs pas grong khyer
14b2(rgyud): gi phyi rol gi char brtor ma chen mo btang bar bya ba dang/yam shing lnga dang/'bru lnga'i bye brag mar la sngags pa'i bye grong gi dbus su zhi ba'i thab khung byas la/'chi ba dang rnam par 'i gzugs su byas pas par
14b3(rgyud): byed do;;gang 'ga' zhig la grong laswogs pa sbyin par 'gyur ba la bzlas pa tsam gis zlog par byed do//rtag tu bzlas na da lha dang lha ma yin dang/lho mche chen po rnams lan bdun
14b4(rgyud): 'gyur ro//dpal rje btsun sangs rgyas bcoM ldan 'das mngon par byang chub pa'i dus su bdud bzhi las/brgyal bar bya ba'i don tu/sngags kyi rgyal po 'di mdzad spu mi snyan pa phyung
14b5(rgyud): ba'i phyir/mdzad spu'i nor bu zhes bya bar grags pa yin no//sog ma la mngon par sngags te/gtor bas/sbrul dag byer bar byed do//khyim gi rtsig so la rdo rgyus la mngon par sngags pas/
14b6(rgyud): bris na;sbrul gyi zil gyis non par mi 'gyur ro//yang rdo leb la sngags bzong bus bkos te/bris la sbas na/ri dgas dang/sbrul bdug pa rnaMs khyim 'am/grong ngam/grong khyer du sbas na ji srid 'dod
14b7(rgyud): pa'i bar du mche ba 'ching bar byed do//phyung bas grol bar 'gyur ro//sngags 'di ma sgrubs su zin kyang las thaMs cad byed par 'gyur ro//gseg ma laswogs pa la yongasu bzlas te gtor
14b8(rgyud): na;mche ba can thaMs cad kyi 'tshe ba 'ching bar byed do//dbang chen kyi dkyil 'khor gis khyab pa'i sna tshogs rdo rje'i dbus su//sgrub par bya ba'i ming dang/bcas pa'i bsngags thaMs
15a1(rgyud): @#//cad bri bar bya'o//dur khrod kyi ro sregs pa'i shing gi phur bu la lan bdun mngon par sngags pa sgo'i steng du sbas na/skyed kyi (mchog)thaMs cad kyi skyed med par byed do//phyung bas glol
15a2(rgyud): bar 'gyur ro//a ni hi ni tsu li/a b+ha yaM ka ri/e lu bA+e lu shi la pa Da/a dzi ha pe lu/A la g+ha sA pa ta tha ra te/i ad taM/rta ri khi li//mo mo hi tham b+hi dz+ha ka reng/Sha hi Sha Tha Sha swahA/bsngag+sa
15a3(rgyud): kyi rgyal po 'di sngags sngar ma smos pa'i las thaMs cad ma sgrubs su zin kyang byed par 'gyur ro//gzhan yang mngon par 'dod pa'i las de dag thams cad byed par the tshom
15a4(rgyud): med do;;lan cha dang skye tshe mnyam pa'i sbyin sreg gam//phub ma'i sbyin sregs kyis pha rol kyi dpung gi tshogs rnaMs 'byed par byed do//am b+ha sim bA kam pA su 'dzo mo
15a5(rgyud): phe ;;e bud pa su/sngags kyi rgyal po 'di ma sgrubs su zin kyang/rtag tu bzlas na 'jigs pa thaMs cad zlog par byed do//rdo rje mchu'i thig les dgra bo thaMs cad rmongs par
15a6(rgyud): byed cing/khros pas rab tu dngang bar byed do//bcings pa las bzlas na 'chi ba rnams grol bar byed do//phongs par gyur pas bzlas na/rnyed pa 'grub par 'gyur ro//e Shod thi to//
15a7(rgyud): hu lu hu lu//dz+wA lA dz+wI ho//hu lu hu lu/ya trA+e bo thi Thi to//hu lu hu lu//had Te ba pra ti//ga tsha tu/hU lU hU lU/swahA//sngags kyi rgyal po 'di 'thab pa dang/'thab mo dang/rnam par rtsod pa thaMs cad la zlas
15a8(rgyud): pa byas pas/zhi bar byed do//phum ma'i sbyin sreg+sa gis kyang thaMs cad du zhi bar 'gyur ro//ji ltar rnyed pa'i me tog la sngags brjod de/chu la skur na thaMs cad du zhi bar 'gyuro zhing/zhi bar
15b1(rgyud): 'gyur zhing/rnam par rgyal ba the tsom med do//grong khyer tshig pa'i me la mngon du phyogs pas 'dug ste/mngon par sngags pa'i chu snyim pa gang gtor na/khyim gang srung bar 'dod pa'i khyim
15b2(rgyud): de srung bar byed cing/mes tshig pa'i me kha las thar bar byed do//rkang gnyis dang rkang bzhi mching ba la phub ma'i sbyin sregs kyis zhi bar 'gyur byed do//oM pad me pad ma a k+Shi pad ma su
15b3(rgyud): b+hag ge /phu phu phu/sngags 'di thal ba la bzlas te bskus bas/mig rgya btab pa (pa ris pa)sos par byed do/byabs pas mig rengs pa sos par byed do//lan bdun mngon par sngags pas mig g+si
15b4(rgyud): khros mthong ma thag rab tu dang bar byed do//lan bdun sngags pa'i chus gdong bkrus na skye bo thams cad dga' bar 'gyur byed do//brtsig pa la (ma)sos pa'i sngags
15b5(rgyud): rdo rgyas kyis bris na/mig nad thaMs cad 'joms par byed do//dben par 'dug ste sems rtse gcig pas skyes bu'i mig gnyis la mngon par sngags pa byas na/gang gi tshe
15b6(rgyud): g.yon pa 'gul bar gyur pa de'i tshe/bse pa'i dgos pa 'grub par ston pa yin no//g.yas pa 'gul bar byed na/mi dge ba'i dogs pa ston par byed do//oM mo tsa ni mo tsa ya/mo k+sha ni mo
15b7(rgyud): k+sha ya/dz+nyi baM ba de swahA//'dis mngon par sngags pa'i til mar gyis skye ba'i sgo la skus na/sbrum ma bde bar btsa' bar byed do//bcings pa la mngon par phyogs par byed pas/lan bdun
15b8(rgyud): mngon par sngags pa'i chu snyim pa bdun mngon par gtor ba dus gsum du byas p+sa/bcings pa rnams grol byed do//gur gum 'am/g+hi 'i wad gis gro ga la mngon par bris te/bcings pa'i
16a1(rgyud): @#//'go bo la btags na/bcings pas grol bar 'gyur ro//brtag tu bzlas na bcings pa grol bar byed do//oM ha ri mar ga Ta na ma swahA sra pA tu/de ba ha da tam/b+han d+ha nad mo tsa na swahA//
16a2(rgyud): rdo rgyus la mngon par sngags pa'i sa'i steng du yang dang yang du bris te/lugs las zlog pas phyis ri mar ga Ta na bcings pas 'ching ba las grol bar byed do//lo ma la ming dang sngagasu bcas pa'i mngon
16a3(rgyud): par bris te/'go bo la btags na bcings pa thaMs cad grol bar 'gyur ro//oM ta ri ta ra ya mo tsa ni ma tsa ya/mo k+sha ni mo k+sha ya/'dzI bang ba ra de swahA//sngags kyi rgyal po 'di legs par bzlas na las
16a4(rgyud): thams cad byed par 'gyur zhing/lan bdun mngon par sngags pa'i tili mar gyis skye ba'i sgo la skus na /sbrum ma bde bar tsa bar 'gyur ro//lan bdun mngon par sdags pa'i chu mthungs na/
16a5(rgyud): mngal 'khyims pa bde bar btsa' bar byed do//rtag tu zlas pas bdag dang gzhan mchi ba las thar bar 'gyur ro//skye ba 'am dpung pa la srung ba btags pas 'jigs pa thaMs cad la srung bar
16a6(rgyud): byed do//oM ta re tud ta re tu re mo k+sha ya 'dzi bam swahA//sngags kyi rgyal po 'di sngar smos pa'i bya ba byed par 'gyur ro//gur gum gi gro ga la gang ga'i ming smos pa'i sngags mngon par bris la/spra chil
16a7(rgyud): la btums te/dri zhi mi pa'i chus bkang ste/bum pa gsar pa'i nang du bcug te/me tog dri zhim po laswogs pas dus gsuM du mchod pa byas na/'thab pa dang/'thab mo dang/rtsod pa thaMs cad las
16a8(rgyud): rnam par rgyal bar 'gyuro//e ran ta ba ne kA ka/gA ga ti/ra mu ba si ta pi pa tu d+hu ta/bA nI yaM bA shAl la b+hA ba tu gur bi ni//lan bdun mngon par sngags pa'i chu 'khor ba gsum po nyar 'ongs
16b1(rgyud): pas mthungs na/sbrum ma bed bar btsa' bar byed do//oM a ma ra ni 'dzi ban ti yA+e swahA//sngags kyi rgyal po 'di legs par zlas na las thaMs cad byed par 'gyur ro//lus la skyes pa'i dug mi
16b2(rgyud): bzad pa dang/spre'u dug dang/sbrul dug laswogs pa'i rma la bung ba dang/a ru ra dang/ru rta laswogs pa la yongasu bzlas te skus na/rang bzhin du gnas par 'gyur ro//nad dang rnam par 'tshe ba
16b3(rgyud): thams cad la sngags 'dud bcings na zhi bar byed/rtag tu bzlas na tshe zad pa yang tshe lo rgya thub par 'gyuro//mar dang til kyi sbyin sregs gis thaMs cad zhi bar byed do//mngon bar sngags
16b4(rgyud): pa'i sman gi btung ba nad thaMs cad las yongasu thar bar byed do//lan rgya bkrus pa'i mar la mngon par sngags pas na ba'i yan lag la skus na/rang bzhin du ga nas par 'gyur ro//
16b5(rgyud): mgo bo'i nad la til mar la sngags te/bskus pas rang bzhin du gnas par gyur ro//gdug pa'i rma la chu la sngags nas rtor bas sos par 'gyur ro//spri ha'i nad la be dang
16b6(rgyud): laswogs pa'i mtshon gyis gtu bas na//spre ha'i bcad par 'gyur ro//na rgyud 'di dang nim pa'i rtsa ba rma bya'i 'jug ma sgro dang gcig tu sbyar la/rgya rtsa rgyad mngon par sngags pa'i gdug
16b7(rgyud): pas bdugs na/rims nad nyin cig pa dang/nyin gnyis pa dang/nyin gsum dang/nyin bzhi pa laswogs pa sel bar byed cing/'byung po dang/sha za dang/bram ze'i sprin po laswogs pa sel
16b8(rgyud): bar 'gyuro//bud med bu shi bsa'i lan rgya rtsa rgyad mngon par sngagasapa'i bum pas khrus byas na/bud bson po dang ldan par 'gyur ro//gro ga la bris pa'i sngags skye ba 'am/dpung pa la btag+sa pas/
17a1(rgyud): @#//sngags rjes su dran pas g.yul ngogs su 'jug na rgyal ba rnyed par 'gyur zhing/byams pas nad thams cad sel byed do//o~M pa ta tsa la na swahA//sngags kyi rgyal po 'di
17a2(rgyud): sngon du snyan pa'i cho gas legs par bsgrubs na/las thos cad byed do//phol mig gi 'jigs pa la mngon par sngags pa'i chu mthungs na/phol mig med par 'gyur ro//gro ga 'am shu gu la
17a3(rgyud): bris te;sgo la btags pas phol mig med par 'gyur no//'di ni 'tshe ba thams cad la mar la sngags pa'i til kyi sbyin sreg gi srung ba chen po yin no//khwa'i sgro lag pas bzung ste bzlas pa
17a4(rgyud): khri byas la gang gi khang pa'i steng gi logasu sbas pa de 'ber bar byed do//o~M bi sha ci par na sha ba ri sarba u pa tra na sha ni swahA//rig pa chen mo 'di ma sgrubasu zin kyang/rkang gnyis pa dang
17a5(rgyud): rkang bzhi pa thams cad kyi rnam par 'tshe ba sel bar byed cing/las thos cad byed par 'gyur ro//sbyin sreg gam/bzlas pa 'am/khrus sam/sngags 'dud dam/mngon par
17a6(rgyud): sngags pa'i chu mthung ba 'am/byabs pas nad thams cad sel bar byed/gro ga la gur gum gis bris pa'i sngags skye ba 'am/dpung pa la btags na rtsod sbyong la rgyal ba thob par 'gyur ro//
17a7(rgyud): rims nyin bzhi pa la sogs pa'i mi bzad pa'i las rnam par grol bar 'gyur ro//srin po la sogs pa'i 'jigs pa sel bar byed cing/'khrug pa dang/rtsod pa dag las rgyal ba grub par byed do//
17a8(rgyud): stag dang/chu sprin dang/lto 'che chen po dang/rkun pa la sogs pa ma mthong zhing/rtag tu bzlas na skye bo thaMs cad dga' bar 'gyur ro//o~M a r+b+ya dri ti ya/a mu kas cag k+nu stam b+ha ya Tha tha swahA//
17b1(rgyud): tshes gang la mig na bar gyur na pa de la rtsig pa 'am/spang leb la rdo rgyus kyis tshes bris la yi ge i kha sbyar gsuM du bya'o//oM can hi ma ha ro sha na hu~M phaT//sngags kyi rgyal po 'di lan gcig zlas
17b2(rgyud): pas;sdig pa thaMs cad nges par sreg par byed do//'byung po la sogs pa'i gdon gis zin pa las/yungs kar la sogs pas gdeg pas/gdon thams cad byer bar byed do//kham bor
17b3(rgyud): la la rdo rgyus kyis bris te sgo la btags pas gsar du btsas pa'i byis pa srung bar byed do//spra tshil gyis gzugs sor bzhi pa byas pa de'i snying khar sgrub bya'i ming dang/bcas pa'i ming sngags
17b4(rgyud): mngon par bris te bcug la/de'i kha la tsher mas btsugs nas;phyir rgol ba'i kha phur btab par 'gyur ro//rkang pa la btsu gas na 'gro ba dang 'ong ba rengs par byed do//snying khar btsug+sa
17b5(rgyud): na 'khro bar 'joms par byed do//ma'i rdzi ngar gi phur pa 'am/lcags kyi phur bus bdab por bya'o//ming nas bzung zhing yan lag dang/gang la phur pa gdab pa de dang de 'jig par
17b6(rgyud): 'gyur ro//gra bo'i sgo'i 'og tu sbas na byer bar byed do//dur khrod kyi thal ba la mngon par sngags pas sgo'i ya them la rtor na 'byer ro//ral gyi la mngon par sngags pasagayul du 'jug na rgyal
17b7(rgyud): ba bsgrub par byed do//bya ba gang la sgrub pa na rtor ma nye bar bstabs na de dang de 'grub par 'gyur ro//sngags pa dge ba 'am/mi dge ba'i las gang 'dod pa'i zlas pa tsam gis byed do//
17b8(rgyud): oM ka shes span da/ku she span da/span na da taM/shu na bya bA+e sma ni ma ma taM tsa/ta tha sba na Da/yA thA span da si badzri na swahA//mig 'gul bar 'gyur na byams pas zhi bar byed do//oM 'dzam b+he mo
18a1(rgyud): @#//he ra hri d+he ya ma bar ta ni hUM phaT swahA//sngags kyi rgyal po 'di tho rangs bya rog bsgra mi grag pa'i dus su//lan bdun mngon par sngags pa'i chus gdong bkrus na skye bo thams cad
18a2(rgyud): dga' bar 'gyur ro//rgyal ba mar dang pad ma dkar po'i skud pa'i sdong bus tu ba blangs la/rgya rtsa rgyad mngon par sngags pa des|des mig la skus pas/bud med thaMs cad yid 'phrog par byedo|
18a3(rgyud): rgyal ba kha mthur du ltas pa'i me tog gi rtsa ba dang//shi ri kha 'ad+ha dag sbyar la rgya rtsa rgyad mngon par sngagasapa byas des/thig ler byas na rje bo 'khros pa rab tu sad par byed do//
18a4(rgyud): ku cho dang/'thab pa dang/rtsod pa dag las rgyal bar 'gyur ro//cho ga bzhin du rtag tu bzlas pas grong khyer bskyod pa skyed par byed do//oM stam b+ha ni/stam b+ha ya/dzam b+ha ni/dzam bA ya/

18a7(rgyud): oM na ma b+hi ma la tsan tra yA//de pa rung tra yA/sad te na d+h+yi ya/te lo ka/sad te na pa ri gri h+ya he//sad t+yan d+ha rma s+thi tho lo ke/sad t+yaM/bram/h+ma sa nA ta nam/sad t+ya bud+d+ha shwa/d+ha rma shwa/g+hu n+na sang ga ra/
18a8(rgyud): a ne ne sad ta ya/bA gaM kye na/k+ni pra ma/bi sha dar ba ne/tad ya thA/oM sa ra sa ra/si ri si ri/su ru su ru/me ru man da ra bra ti/kA shwa/a bi sha/kA+e lA sA/kU ta pu trA yA/na ma ya ma ya tsin ti
18b1(rgyud): tam ka r+ya ma/tad swa rba ma gru hi ta ta ta/ad mA r+tha ma 'ba'/pa r+tha ma/shu b+ham bA/yi da ba a shu b+ham/nang par brtsang ma'i sa phyogasu dkyil 'khor byas shing bum pa gzhag la/lha'i bu dri ma med pa'i zla ba la
18b2(rgyud): mchod pa rgya che ba byas nas/me long la mngon par sngags te/khye'u 'am bu mo legs par khrus byas la/gos legs par khrus byas pas bskon cing/tsan dan kar po'i mi d+he gus yan lag byugs la/
18b3(rgyud): me tog dri zhim pa'i 'phreng ba 'gul tu btags pa/grong pa'i chos kyis bar tu ma nyams pa la stan na/ji ltar bsams pa'i dgos pa phyin ci ma log par ston par 'gyur ro//dgongs
18b4(rgyud): sngas drung du dkyil 'khor nye bar byugs la/bcom ldan 'das dang lha'i bu dri ma med pa'i zla ba la mchod pa rgya chen po byas la/rgya rtsa rgyad zlas nas cang mi smra bar nyal na/
18b5(rgyud): dge ba dang mi dge ba ji ltar bsams pa bzhin bston par 'gyur ro//brtag tu zlas na 'jig rten gi spyod pa ston par 'gyur ro//oM shra ba na pi sha tsi ni mun te swahA//ba ru ra'i shing ngam n+ya gro
18b6(rgyud): ta'i shing gi rtsa bar 'dug la/cang mi smra bar 'bum phrag gcig bzlas na/'grub par 'gyur te/'jig rten gsuM gi spyod pa yang ston par 'gyur ro//yang na legs par khrus byas la/gos rtsang ma
18b7(rgyud): gos te/dben par 'dug la khri bzlas pas/'grub par 'gyur ro//dgongs kha nyal ka ru brta la rgya rtsa rgyad mngon par bzlas nas gdong dang rkang pa gnyis bkrus nas/rmi lam du dge ba dang mi dge
18b8(rgyud): ba rnam par ston par 'gyur ro//oM na mo sA tA nAm/samyag ka sa b+hud d+ha/kA+o ti nAmA/tad t+ya thA/oM tsa le tsu la/btsun te ma ha b+hid de t+ya/sad t+ya bA ti ni/bA ra de ka tha ya/ka tA yA swahA//gong du bshad
19a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i cho gas me long ngam/ral dri 'am/mar me 'am/chu snod la ston par byed do//lan rgya rtsa rgyad mngon par sngags pa'i chus gdong bkrus la/mi smra bar nyal na rmi lam
19a2(rgyud): du dge ba dang mi dge ba ston par 'gyur ro//oM mu tsu li swahA//mo hA ni swahA//tad ti li swahA//gsang sngags 'di khri zlas na grub par 'gyur ro//btso ldan 'das 'phags pa spyan ras gzig+sa
19a3(rgyud): dbang phyug la mchod pa rgya chen po byas nas/lan rgya rtsa rgyad bzlas nas mngon par sngags pa'i gdong chus bkrus te/dgos pa yid la byed pa'i bzlas pas mi smra bar nyal na/bcom ldan

19a5(rgyud): po;'di lan 'brum phrag bcu bzlas na 'grub par 'gyuro//gong du smos pa'i cho gas dge ba dang/mi dge ba rmi lam du ston par 'gyuro//oM na ma sa man ta b+hud d+ha nan/a pra ti
19a6(rgyud): ha ta sha sa na nam/tad ya thA/ku ma ra ru pi Na dar sha ya/dar sha ya/Ad mA nam/pi b+hu ti ma sa mun b+ha wa ya/rtsa ba nam ni be da ya/ya tha b+hu ta hUM hUM//brje btsun 'jaM dpal gzhon

19b1(rgyud): de nyid du mi smra bar 'dug ste/sha dang nya sha dang /ma sha dang /'dzaM bu'i kling du mngon par sngags pa'i rtor ma nye bar stabs la/dus gsuM du sngags zlas na/zhag dang po la mtshan ma nye bar ston

19b5(rgyud): mchod pa byas na/nyal ba'i dus sa rgya rtsa rgyad bzlas te/bdag gang nas skyes par 'gyur/gang du skye bar 'gyur zhes yid la bsaM shing /nyal na 'das pa dang ma 'ongs pA dang

20a1(rgyud): @#//oM sa ba na bi lo ka ni sid d+ha lo tsa ni sa ba nam/me ka ta ya swahA/sngags kyi rgyal po 'di gong nas smos pa'i las thams cad byed par 'gyur ro//A+oM a did ti de ba dad tAM/me ti hi/
20a2(rgyud): da d+ha pa ya swahA//chu'i nad du zhugs te bu mo gang gi ched du zlas pa byed pa de de la ster bar byed do//yi ge 'buM zlas pa byas pa kri yang ku'i me tog gam/pa la sha'i me tog gi sbyin sreg gi 'buM

20a4(rgyud): bu'i sbyin sreg 'buM gis/rgyal rigasu skye ba rgyal rig srid rnyed par 'gyur zhing /gzhan dag gis dpal chen po thob par 'gyur ra ro//ji lta ba bzhin du rnyed pa'i me tog rnams kyis//sbyin sreg

20a7(rgyud): skor ba;bcom ldan 'das ba su da ra dang bcas pa/gser kyi mdog can/ne'u le dang/sa bon kyis bkang ba phyag na snim pa/gsus pa 'chang ba/rgyan thaMs cad kyis rgyan pa/gong du
20a8(rgyud): bshad pa'i gnod sbyin mo dang/gnod sbyin gi dbang po dang bcas pa la dus gsum du mchod pa byas nas/sgos shing bzlas pa byas nas/bcoM ldan 'das kyis ('jig rten)gsum gi rgyal srid stsol
20b1(rgyud): bar mdzad do//tho rangs chu khyor rgya rtsa rgyad byin pa dang/bya rog skad mi sgrag pa'i chu'i nang du zhugs te/bzlas pa byed pa gang yin pa de ni//gter mi zad cing 'phel bar 'gyur ro//yang
20b2(rgyud): na gser laswogs pa'i byang bu la/ba su d+ha ra gnod sbyin ma dang bcas pa dang/byang bu gnyis pa bcom ldan 'das 'dzam b+ha la gnod sbyin skor ba/yang dag pa bris sa la/kha sbyar du byas te/

20b4(rgyud): //!//le'u gnyis pa'o//!//!//oM badzra ti k+Sh+Na du kha tshe da pradz+nya gnya' mur ta ye/nya na ka ya ba ki shwa ra/a ra pa tsa na/yA te na ma/bcoM ldan 'das 'jam pa'i
20b5(rgyud): rdo rje dpa'o gcig bu grub pa/ston ka'i zla ba'i 'dog ltar kar ba/phyag g.yon ud pa la sngon po snams pa/g.yas kyis mchod sbyin par bdag nyid bsams le/A+oM a r+ya na pa na
20b6(rgyud): pra kri ti pra b+ha swa ra/su bi shud d+he/d+ha rma d+ha tu badzra swa b+ha ba ad mo ko hA/zhag nyi shu rtsa gcig gis dbyangs can gi ngag thob par 'gyur zhing/zla ba drug gis ngag gi dbang phyug 'grub par
20b7(rgyud): byed do/bcom ldan 'das ngag gi dbang phyug mngon gsuM du mthong zhing thos pas 'dzin pa yang thob par 'gyur ro//shu dag zho phyed kyi chad la lan rgya rtsa rgyad mngon par sngags pa/
20b8(rgyud): tho rangs 'o ma 'am/mar ram/til mar dang/zla ba gcig mthungs na/glen pa dang/dig pa dang/med par 'gyur ro//khu phyug dar ma'i skad dang/'dra bar dbyangs snyan cing chig gsal
21a1(rgyud): @#//bar 'gyur ro//bzla ba drug goms par byas na ma thos pa'i bstan chos mngon du 'don du 'gyur zhing/thos pa nam yang mi 'gyur te/thos pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//oM bag
21a2(rgyud): kye tan ma/rje btsun gsung gi dbang phyug gi snying po 'di gong ma bzhin/ting nge 'dzin la gnas pas goms par byas na/gong du bshad pa'i las thos cad byed par 'gyur ro//rgya mtsho
21a3(rgyud): 'gro ba'i chu'o la skye ba tsam gi chu la rgal la/'dza ta'i me tog gi phud la me tog 'bum phrag gcig bzlas nas sngags brjod cing /phud la re re rgyun phyogs su 'bab par 'gyur ba
21a4(rgyud): lags|phud pa gcig brgyun las zlas par 'ong ba de so la ma reg par mid na/thos pa 'dzin pa la sogs par 'dzad do//ji lta ba bzhin rnyed pa'i me tog gi sbyin
21a5(rgyud): sreg 'bum phrag bzhi byas na/ngag gi dbang phyug dang mtshungs par 'gyur ro//sman 'ab+h+ra ma'i phye ma zho gnyis tsam la lan rgya rtsa rgyad zlas la/tho rangs dkyil 'khor
21a6(rgyud): bcas te|ci 'byor pas bco ldan 'das 'jam pa'i rdo rje la yang dag par mchod de/mngar ra ma/skyur mthungs na/zla ba drug gis thos pa 'dzin par 'gyuro//tshig gi bdag po'i dbyangs
21a7(rgyud): snyan par yang skye bar 'gyur ro//sngon du snyen pa'i cho gas yi ge 'bum zlas na/sngags kyi rgyal po 'grub par 'gyur ro//phyis bzla ba bza'as zin pa dang/nyi ma bzas zin pa na/lcags
21a8(rgyud): las byas pa'i ral dri lag par blangs te/zla ba mi snang ba de srid grol bar 'gyur ba de srid zlas pa byas pa na/ral dra'i rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//de bzhin 'khor lo dang/rdo rje dang/rtse gsu
21b1(rgyud): pa dang /mda' dang /tho ba dang /zhags pa laswogs par sgrub par bya zhing /rig pa laswogs pa dang /bcud kyis len pa laswogs pa 'dus byas pa bsgrub par bya'o//yang na mig smin nam/
21b2(rgyud): thig le 'am/dbang du byas pa'i brdzas bsdus te/sgrub par bya'o//'o ma mthung ba dang /zho mthung ba dang /mar dang bcas pa/kha ra dang bcas pas snod bkang ste/lag pas bkab nas zlas pa
21b3(rgyud): byas ste/'thungs na lo lnga rgya 'tsho bar 'gyur ro//ma sha khar bcug nas bzlas pa byas te/mid na/sdeb sbyor dang thos pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//smra mkhas pa dang /yid zhungs par 'gyur ro//
21b4(rgyud): pad ma'i sbyin sreg 'bum gis bco ldan 'das mngon gsum du 'gyur ro//byil pa'i sbyin sreg 'bum gis 'jig rten du gsum gi rgyal srid 'grub par byed do//ci lta ba bzhin
21b5(rgyud): du rnyed pa'i me tog gi sbyin sreg gis gang gi dngos grub thob par 'gyur ro//mar la sngags pa'i 'bru lnga'i sbyin sreg 'bum phrag bzhi byas pa tsam gis/gnod sbyin mo dang/glu'i
21b6(rgyud): bu mo laswogs pa 'gugs par byed do//de bzhin du yam shing lngas kyang bya'o//rtag tu goms par byas na/tshe 'di nyid la sangs rgyas nyid kyang 'grub par byed par 'gyur ro//o~M hri hU~M//
21b7(rgyud): sngags kyi rgyal po 'i 'bras bu mnyaM por ster bar byed do//o~M hri ma ha la yang ge maha lA yang ge ma ha sa ra sat+yai na ma/rje btsun 'phags ma sgrol ma'i snying po 'di las thaMs cad byed par 'gyuro//
21b8(rgyud): o~M bra tis ti pa ra shud d+ha/sarba d+ha rma pra kri t+ya/pa ri shud d+ho hU~M/sa pa h+ya/a b+h+ya yan ta ra si ta/zhes bya bas rgyu ba dang/mi rgyu ba dang bcas pa'i sna tshogs pa dang/bdag nyid rang bzhin gsi
22a1(rgyud): @#//yongs su dag par bsams la/pad ma kar po khru bdun pa'i tshad dang/de'i steng du zla ba'i dkyil 'khor/de'i steng du phyag gnyis pa pad ma dang/mchog sbyin gis rnam p+Ra

22a3(rgyud): ga'i bzla ba ltar kar ba/tsan dan kar po'i d+hi kus byugs shing/me tog gis mdzes par rgyan pa/nam za' kar po dang/mu tig gi 'phreng ba thugs kha mdzes par byas pa/sku rin po che sna

22a6(rgyud): du dran pa;gong tu smos pa'i yon tan thams cad kyis mdzes pa'i lha mo rnam par bsam par bya'o//de'i 'og tu lte ba'i phyogs su bzla ba'i dkyil 'khor gi dbus su yi ge oM 'dog kar
22a7(rgyud): po rnam par bsams la/de las byung ba'i dag gi rang bzhin ma lus pa'i 'phreng ba rgyun mi 'chad par 'byung bar rnam par bsaMs zhing/gcig bu der phyogs pa'i yid can sngags pas/

22b5(rgyud): sngags bcas bzos nas ni//zla ba drug gis thos 'dzin byed//bzla ba gsum gis tshig bdag 'gyur//zla ba gcig gis blo ldan zhing //khu phyug gzhon nu'i skad bzhin du//dbyangs
22b6(rgyud): snyan pa'i ni skye bar 'gyur//gangs can skyes pa'i a ru 'am//gangs can skyes pa'i shu dag la//tho rangs lan bdun mngon sngags nas//zla cig zos nas zla de la//tshig bdag po deng yid
22b7(rgyud): gzhungs dang//dbyangs snyan shes rab dang ldan dang//skye bar 'gyur zhing zla drug gis//thos pa 'dzin par yang 'gyur ro//oM bAg bA di ni//bag tsa me ni yang tsha//
22b8(rgyud): sa ra swa sti/ma he shA ri swahA//pad ma las skyes pa'i kar po chen po'i bzungs 'di ni//gong du bshad pa'i las thaMs cad byed par 'gyur ro//oM ar ka ma li ni/ki Ni ki Ni/k+Shi Ni
23a1(rgyud): @#//k+Shi ni;swo hA//bcom ldan 'das dpa'o gcig pu sgrub pa gsung gis/rang bzhin ma lus pa'i pu ti snams pa/phyag g.yas kyis shes rab kyi ral gri 'char ba/gzhon nu'i
23a2(rgyud): gzugs can 'jam pa'i rdo rje bdag nyid bsaMs la/mdun du nyi ma'i phreng ba dbyangs can chen mo dang//rgyab du dpal chen mo dang//g.yas su skra can ma dang/g.yon du nye ba skra can ma rnam par bsaM par

23a4(rgyud): rim pas/'a bum phrag bzhi bzlas la//de nas dbyangs can kyi mar ram/b+h+ra ma'i mar srang cig tsam la yongs su bzlas ste/bdag nyid la dbyangs can gi phyag gis rab tu rtsol bar yongasu
23a5(rgyud): brtsol rtog cing//ting nge 'dzin la gan+sa pas btung bar bya'o//de la b+h+ra ma'i mar 'di ni//b+h+ra ma'i khu ba 'o ma dang//mar srang sum bcu rtsa gnyis dang//yung ba dang ma la ti dang//dur rjid a ru rar
23a6(rgyud): bcas pa;;de dag cha ni srang re dang//pi pi ling dang bi ti ka/sen r+Sha ba dang ka rang bcas//de dag srang gi bzhi ba char shes//sman rnams dag ni btags nas kyang//de la 'di dag lugs byas la/
23a7(rgyud): de dag thaMs cad cha gnyam pa//me chu ngu la de skol bya//de nas mthungs pa tsam gis ni//bla med ngag ni dag 'gyur dang//'tshams bdun nye bar long spyod pas//mi 'am ci dang skad 'tshungs 'gyur;
23a8(rgyud): zla gsum nye bar long spyod pas//mi ni shes rab gan+si su 'gyur//zla gsuM rab tu sbyor ba yis//thos pa mchog ni 'dzin par 'gyur//zla ba drug gis mngos gsu tu//mi na ngag dbang 'tshungs

23b5(rgyud): stong pa nyid mngon gsum du byas nas/pad ma dkar po'i steng du zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng na bzhugs pa sku 'dog dkar po ral gri dang/po ti snams par bdag nyid yud tsam yongs su
23b6(rgyud): grub par rnam par bsaMs la/ngag gi dbang phyug bdag nyid kyi g.yas su skra can dang/g.yon du nye ba'i skra can ma rin po che'i pad ma snaMs pa/sku 'dog dkar po rnam par bsam mo//'dun

26b1(rgyud): byas pa'i 'khor lo la skud pa dmar pos bcings pa na/sba tshil kyis rang bzhin can gyi gzugs kyi snying po ka'i dbusu bcug la/seng ldeng gi 'dab ma la dus gsuM du sros na/rkang pas 'gugs par byedo//
26b2(rgyud): skyes pa 'am bud med dbang du byed na/zangs kyi ma'i khab kyis rkang pa phug la/spros pas dbang du 'gyuro//yig ge yang las yongs su 'gyur ba'i rlung gis dkyil 'khor yongasu bsaMs la/de'i steng du yongs
26b3(rgyud): su gnas pa'i sgrub par bya ba skra ma bcings shing/lcer bu 'gren pa zhags pas bcings pas snying ka la lcags skyus snun pa rlung gi shugs can kyi rigs sngags kyis thaMs cad grangs te/sgrub pa po'i
26b4(rgyud): drung zhing bya ba thaMs cad rnaMs par bsaMs par bya'o//goms par byas pa namakha' la 'gren par yang sgoms pa tsam gis 'gyuro//mar dang/sbrang rtsi dang/kha ra'i nang du sngags
26b5(rgyud): gcug la;dus gsum du me tog dri zhim po la swogs pas/mngon par mchod cing/sngags zlas na gang 'dod pa de dbang du 'gyuro//pad ma dkar po'i skud pa'i sdong po byas la/ba ser
26b6(rgyud): skya'i mar kyis so ma btang ba'i khab phor la/du ba chags pa la rgya rtsa rgyad mngon par sngags pas mig la skus na skye 'o thaMs cad dga' bar 'gyuro//du khrod du ka pa la chen po la pad ma dkar po'i skud
26b7(rgyud): pa'i sdong bu dang/snum chen pos mar ngo'i tshes rgyang dam/bcu bzhi'i nu+ib mo du ba sgrub par byas la/des mig la skus na thaMs cad dga' bar 'gyaro//a ma le bi ma le/kuM kum me/sa ma ye na/b+had
26b8(rgyud): b+ho si ta/bI dun id tsha yA de bor bar Sha ti bid d+ho ta ya ti/gardza ta;bi sma yA ma hA rA dzem/sa me ti bar d+ha yan taM/de be b+h+yo//ga d+har be b+h+yo//shi ki gru hA de pA/a nan ta s+ya gra ha Ni sa/a ka na ma na
27a1(rgyud): @#//ya kra pa na ma dzu hA+o ma swahA//sngags kyi rgyal po 'dis snyen pa'i cho ga byas la/khang pa'i 'dun gi dbus ba'i lji bas byugs la/gru bzhir bris ste/dur ba'i rtsal bkram nas me 'bir
27a2(rgyud): la;a rga'i me tog rgya rtsa rgyang blangs nas/sngags zlas shing me tog re re me la sbyin sreg byas na/'dod pa'i skyes pa 'am/bud med 'gugs pa sgrub par 'gyur zhes/mkhas pas de ltar shes par
27a3(rgyud): byas la sgrub par bya'o////dpa' bo gcig pu sgrub pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las////le'u bzhi pa rdzogas+ho////rgya gar kyi mkhan po chen po ti pad ka ra shri gnya' na dang /
27a4(rgyud): lo tsha ba dge slong dge ba'i blo gros kyis sgyur ba/dge slong tshul khris rgyal bas rtan la phab pa 'o//!//na ma swA man d+ha b+hud d+ha nam/a tra ti ha ta sha ka na nam/
27a5(rgyud): tad yA thA;oM ga ga na ga ga Na g+han dze/a na ya swa rba la hu la hu/sa ma ya ma nu smra ra a kar sha nam/mi ba lam pa/ya te spri tam me/saM pa da ya swahA//bcom ldan 'das 'od
27a6(rgyud): srungs kyi 'o//oM ma ni badzre hUM/hrid ya ba man tra/oM ni d+hi ra hUM phaT/u pa hrid ya man tra/swarba bdi swahA//oM badzra/oM na mo b+ha ga wa te/swarba dur ka ti pa ri sho d+ha ni rA tse ye/ta tha g+ha ta ya/a ra ha te/
27a7(rgyud): sa myag swaM b+hud d+ha ya/tad t+ya thA/swarba pa pam b+hi sho d+ha ne/a sho d+ha ne/sho d+ha ne/swarba kar rma a pa ra na b+hi shud d+he swahA/oM swarba b+hid swarba a pa ra na b+hi sho d+ha na ya ha na hung phaT/oM swarba b+hid huM/oM swarba b+hid
27a8(rgyud): phaT;oM swarba bdi a/oM swarba b+hid Ta ta//oM swarba bdi oM/oM swarba bdi swa/oM swarba bdi a/o swarba bdi hUM/oM swarba bi tra ta/oM sha/oM b+ha/oM sho d+ha ne/sho d+ha ni/swarba pa paM b+hi sho d+ha ni shud
27b1(rgyud): d+he b+hi shud d+he/ma maM swarba ka rma a pa ra na b+hi shud d+he ku ru swahA//oM ma ra ni dzi pan ti ye swahA//rig pa 'di ni las thaMs cad byed pa'i bzungaso//oM he he Sh+thi ti sh+hi/b+han d+ha b+han d+ha/d+ha ra ya d+ha ra ya//ni run
27b2(rgyud): run d+ha ni run d+ha/lha sbyin ur n+tsa ma ni swAhA//mdzod spu nor bu'i 'o//na ma sa man d+ha b+hud d+ha nam/swa rba swa mun ga ta nam/na ma kre ta t+ya ka b+hud d+ha ar+ya shrA ba ka nam/na ma bod ti sa ta nam/da sha b+hu mi pra
27b3(rgyud): ta Sh+thi te sha ra nam/b+hod ti swa tA naM//ma hA swa tA naM//tad yA thA;;oM kha kha kha hi kha hI//du Sh+Tha sa tA na ma kra a sa mu sa la pa ra shu shA stA/tsa dur bu dza/tsa dur mu kha/Sha ta tsa ra na/gad tsha gad tsha/
27b4(rgyud): swa rba b+ha g+hi b+hi g+ha ta ka/bi kri ta nan/swarba b+hu ta b+h+ya ka ra/a d+ha hA swa nI/da ne b+h+ya g+h+ra tsa rma ni bi sa na/ku ru ku ru swarba d+ha rma/nam/tshin d+ha tshin d+ha/swarba man tra na/b+hin d+ha b+hin d+ha/pa ra mu tran ;
27b5(rgyud): a kar sha ya/a kar sha ya/swarba pu tran/nir ma tha nir ma tha/swarba du Sh+Ta na/pra pa sha ya/pra pe sha ya/pra pe sha ya/ma d+ha la mad/bA+ai ba sa twa/dzi bi ta na ta ka ra/kur ru kur ru/
27b6(rgyud): ma ma swarba ka t+ya yam/d+ha ha d+ha ha/pa tsa pa tsa/ma b+hi laM pa/sa ma ya ma nu sma ra/hU~M hU~M phaT/pa spo ta ya/spo ta ya/swarba sha ri pu ra ka/de b+ha ga ban kin tsa ri ya/si ma ma swarba r+tha mas d+ha ya/'di ni bcoM ldan
27b7(rgyud): 'das 'jaM dpal gzhon nur gyur pa'i 'khro bo gshin rje gshed ces bzungs kyi rtog pa'i rgyal po rdzogas+ho////@#//rgya gar skad du/badzrar b+hi d+ha ra na mo d+ha ra ni/bod skad du/rdo rjes rnam par 
27b8(rgyud): 'joMs pa zhes bya ba'i gzungs//sangs rgyas dang byang chub seMs dpa' thaMs cad la phyag 'tshalo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus cig na/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje la bzhugs te/sangs rgyas kyi mthus
28a1(rgyud): @#//lag na rdo rjes rab tu smraso lus thaMs cad rdo rjer byin g+si rlabs nas/rdo rje'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugaso//de nas lag na rdo rjes sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis
28a2(rgyud): rlabs gyis dang///byang chub seMs dpa' thaMs cad kyis byin kyis rlabs kyis/rdo rje 'khro bo las byung ba/rdo rje snying pos rab tu smras te mi chod pa mi shigs pa/bden pa/sra ba/brtan pa/thaMs
28a3(rgyud): cad du thogs pa med pa/thaMs cad du ma 'pham pa/seMs can thaMs cad skrag par byed pa/sems can thaMd 'jigs par byed pa/rigs sngags thaMs cad gcod par byed pa/rigs sngagasathaMs cad
28a4(rgyud): gnon par byed pa/las thaMs cad 'joms par byed pa/gzhan gyi las thaMs cad 'jig par byed pa/gdon thaMs cad las thar bar byed pa/'byung po thaMs cad 'gugs par byed pa/
28a5(rgyud): rigs sngags kyi las thaMs cad byed du 'jug pa/ma grub pa rnaMs grub par byed pa/grub pa rnaMs chud mi za bar byed pa/'dod pa thaMs cad rab tu sbyin pa/seMs can thaMs can srung ba zhi ba
28a6(rgyud): rgyas pa;sems can thaMs cad rengs par byed pa/rmugs par byed pa'i/gsang sngags kyi mthu chen po 'dis sangs rgyas kyi mthus lag na rdo rjes rab tu smraso//na mo rad na tra ya ya/na ma tsan d+ha badzra
28a7(rgyud): kro ta ye ma hA yak+sha se na pa ta ye/tad ya thA/a traM Ta tra Ta/tro Ta ya tro Ta ya/swu ta swu ta/s+pho Ta ya/s+pho Ta ya/g+hu rna g+hu rna/g+hu na pa ya g+hu rna pa ya/swarba swa twa ni/b+ho Ta ya b+ho Ta ya/saM b+ho tra ya/saM b+ho Ta ya/
28a8(rgyud): b+h+ra ma b+h+ra ma/saM b+h+ra ma ya saM b+h+ra ma ya/swarba b+hud ta ni/ku Ta ku Ta/sad ku Ta ya sang ku Ta ya/swarba shag krung/ka Ta ka Ta/sang ka Ta ya/sang ka Ta ya/sang ku Ta ya/swarba byid t+ya/badzra badzra//s+pho Ta ya badzra badzra//
28b1(rgyud): ka Ta badzra badzra/ma Ta badzra badzra//ma tha badzra badzra/a tra hA swa ni la badzra/su badzra ye swahA//he phul ni ru phul lu/g+h+ri han ku lu/mi li tsu lu/ku ru ku lu/badzra b+hi tsa ya swahA/ki li ki la ya swahA//
28b2(rgyud): ka Ta ka Ta/ma Ta ma Ta/ra Ta ra Ta/mo tra ni Ti mo Ta na ye swahA//tsa ra ni tsa ra ni/ha ra ha ra/sa ra sa ra/ma ra ya badzra b+hi da ra ni swahA/chin d+ha chin d+ha/b+hin d+ha b+hin d+ha/ma ha ki li ki la ya swaha//b+han d+ha b+han d+ha/
28b3(rgyud): kro d+ha kro d+ha/ki li ki la ya swa hA/tsu ru tsu ru/tsan Ta ki li ki la ya swatA/tra sa ya tra sa ya badzra ki li ki la ya swAhA/ha ra ha ra/badzra d+ha ra ya swahA/pra ha d+ha pra ha ra/badzra pra b+ha dz+nya na ya swAhA//
28b4(rgyud): ma ti sh+ti ra badzra/su ti sh+hi ra badzra/tra ti sh+Ti ra badzra/ma hA badzra/a pra tI hA ta badzra/a mo g+ha ya badzra/e hi badzra/shi krang badzra ya swahA/d+ha ra d+ha ra/d+ha ri d+hi ri/d+hu ru d+hu ru/
28b5(rgyud): sarba badzra a par ta ya swahA/a mu ka ma ra ya phaT/na ma sa man ta badzrar nan/sarba badzra sa lan a pa rdza ya ma ha b+ha le/ka ta le/tad ta le/a tsa le/ma d+ha la ma hA+e/a ti
28b6(rgyud): badzra/ma ha ma ba le be ka ra na/a dza ti dza la dza la/ti Ti ti Ti/ting ga le/d+ha ha d+ha ha/te ts+tsho ba ti/ti li ti li/b+han d+ha b+ha d+ha/ma ha ba le/badzra g+hu sha dza la ya swahA/na mo rad na tra ya yA/na mo t+sa badzra
28b7(rgyud): pa ne ye/ma ha yak+sha se na pa ta ye/tad ya thA/oM ha ra ha ra badzra/ma tha ma tha badzra/d+hu na d+hu na badzra/da ha da ha badzra/pa tsa pa tsa badzra/ha ra ya ha ra ya badzra/d+ha ru na d+ha ru na badzra/chin d+ha chin d+ha
28b8(rgyud): badzra/b+hin d+ha b+hin d+ha badzra/hU~M hU~M phaT/na ma tsa sh+nan ta badzra kro ta ye//hu lu hu lu/ti Sh+Ta ti Sh+Ta/b+han d+ha b+han d+ha/ha na ha na/e smri te hu~M phaT////sdig pa thaMs cad sbyang byas nas/sdug bsngal thaMs
29a1(rgyud): @#//cad med par byas/rgyud kun mchog gi rtsa ba ste/dpal kun kyis ni legs par rgyan//sems can dbang po nyams pa dang/tshe zad pa dang tshe nyaMs dang/phun sum tshogs
29a2(rgyud): min gang chag+sa dang/lha rnaMs rgyab kyis 'phyogs pa dang//mdza' sdus skye bo sdang ba dang//bre ze laswogs rtses pa dang//phan tshun mthun pa ma yin dang//nor sgrib pas ni gnod pa dang//mya ngan
29a3(rgyud): mngal nas gnod pa dang/'jigs pa dang ni 'phongs pa dang//gza' dang rgyu skar byad sdems dang//mi zad gdon gis nyen pa'i//rmi laM sdig pa mthong na yang//de ni rab krus rtsang ma
29a4(rgyud): yin;;mdo sde dam pa gnyan par bya//gang dag yid dge sems dang zhing//rtsang ma'i gos kyis rab rgyan te//zab mo'i sangs rgyas spyod yul ba//mdo sde dam pa nyan byed pa/
29a5(rgyud): mdo sde 'di'i gzi rjid kyis/srog chags dag ni thaMs cad kyi/mi zad pa'i nad rnaMs kyang//de dag thaMs cad zhi ba ltar//tshe dang bsod naMs 'phel bar 'gyur//sdig pa kun las rnaM
29a6(rgyud): par thar;;nor bu yungs dkar dur ba dang//rin chen dma' med tsan tan bcas//shel dang rdo rje me tog dang//chu'i gser ram yang na ni//dngul kyi bum pa dag kyang rung/rtsang ma'i gos kyis bkris
29a7(rgyud): shing gang lan grangs nyi shu rtsa gcig gam//yang na lan grangs rgya rtsa rgyad//rdo rje rnam 'joms zlas nas ni//rgyal po rtag tu khrus gyis cig/rdo rje rnam par 'joMs pa zhes bya ba'i gzungs

29b3(rgyud): gshegs pa dgra bcoM pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas thos cad la phyag 'tshal lo//na mo rad na shi kha ni/ta thA ga ta yA/a ra ha te sam myag/saM b+hud ta yA/tad yA thA/rad ne rad ne/
29b4(rgyud): rad ne shi khye ne swahA////sngags 'chi ba'i tshe dran na/ngan song kun las rnam par grol bar 'gyuro//shi 'phos na yang suM bcu rtsa gsuM gi gnam gi lhar skye'o//de bzhin
29b5(rgyud): gshegs pa rin chen rtsa gaphud can kyi mtshan rjesu dran pa'i bzungs/rdzogas+ho//rgya gar skad du//ar+ya badzra a tsi ta/a na la pra mo ha ni/na ma d+ha ra ni /bod skad du/
29b6(rgyud): 'phags pa rdo rje mi 'pham pa me ltar rmongs byed ces bya ba'i gzungs//sangs rgyas dang byang chub seMs dpa' thes cad la phyag 'tshalo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus cig na/bcoM
29b7(rgyud): ldan 'das rin po'i rtse mo la khang pa rtsegs pa can gi'i ri'i gangs ri'i rtse sul gi mchog/ri rab gi mchog dang/rkyed ltar gser gi rdo sna tshogs kyis gang zhing/re 'ur bres par
29b8(rgyud): gyur pa;rin po che'i rdo du ma 'od sna tshogs 'di lta bu byung ba'i rdo leb yangs pa/me tog dri zhim po bkraM zhing/phye ma dang bdug pa dri zhim pa mang po phug gi nang nags sgos pa/gdug+sa dang/
30a1(rgyud): a ka ru dang /phye ma dang /bril bu dang /bla re mdzes par byas pa'i ri bo mchog la bzhugs te/de nas bco ldan 'das lha rnaMs dang /lha'i bu rnaMs dang /lha ma yin rnaMs

30a3(rgyud): po rnaMs dang /log 'dren rnaMs dang /sha za rnams dang /snang ba dang mi snang ba'i ma mo'i tshogs khyab par byed pa/rgya chen po dag gsi dpag tshad lnga rgyar 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor du gyur par

30a6(rgyud): bu rnaMs dang /rnaM namakha' lding rnaMs dang /klu rnaMs dang /gnod sbyin rnaMs dang /mi 'am ci rnaMs dang /srin po rnaMs dang /dri za rnams dang /rig sngags 'chang grub pa rnaMs dang /'byung po rnams dang /
30a7(rgyud): log 'dren rnaMs dang /sha za rnaMs dang /snang ba dang /mi snang ba'i mi mo'i tshogs rnaMs dang /'byung po'i tshogs rnaMs rnam par rgyu zhing /ku co dang /'jigs pa dang /nyaMs thag pa'i sgra 'byin to//

30b1(rgyud): 'jigs pa dang/nyams thag pa dang/'khrug pa'i sgra 'byin/de dag gis gsol pa dang/bcoM ldan 'das bdag cag ni dbang du bgyis te/gnod sbyin pa dang/drang bas bkug cing drangs te/
30b2(rgyud): tho 'tshams na/bcom ldan 'das skyab tu gsol/de skad ces gsol pa dang/bcoM dan 'das kyis thaMs cad kyi steng gi dkyil 'khor yangs pa ston pa/mchog gi blo gros dang/blo dang las dang/
30b3(rgyud): las kyi mtha' dang ldan pa/sa chen po las sa gzhan thob pa/rgyal mtshan du nye ste/chos kyi rgyal srid la dbang skur ba'i/byang chub seMs dpa' seMs dpa' chen po/rjesu dgongs pa tsam gis
30b4(rgyud): 'ongs te;rdo rje zab mo ltar dpung 'go g.yas pa/rdo rje ltar 'khre/gas pa'i gcod pan 'od zer 'tsher ba can/rdo rje'i dpung rgyan dang ldan pa/lus 'khregs shing bral ba/lag na
30b5(rgyud): rdo rje dang zhags pa dang/lag cha rtan po 'bar ba thogs pa/rdo rje phur bu sra bas rab tu 'joMs par byed pa/mthu che ba g.yog bzhi 'buM lnga khri dang ldan pa/'jig rten gi khaMs 'di
30b6(rgyud): kun tu snang bar byed cing/rdo rje rigs kyi gzho nu dang/rdo rje'i sde dang/rdo rje snying po dang/rdo rje tho ba dang/rdo rje snang ba dang/rin po che'i rdo rje dang/gsang ba'i rdo rje dang/'khor lo'i rdo rje laswogs pa
30b7(rgyud): dang;rdo rje'i dmag dpon chen po rnaMs dang/dmag skyong rnaMs dang/rdo rje rig pa'i rgyal po yang dag pa dang/yang dag pa ma yin pa/yang dag par rtogs pa rnaMs dang/rdo rje'i yum rnaMs dang/rdo rje

31a1(rgyud): @#//'dus pa thaMs cad la/kun tu snang bar byed rnam par snang bar byed/rnam par gsal bar byed/rab tu dga' bar byed/yang dag par skul bar byed/so sor skul byed zhing/
31a2(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste/bla gos phud nas zhabs la gcod pan gis gtugs te/lan gsuM gsuM skor ba byas nas/thal mo sbyar te/phyogs gcig tu 'dugo/de nas bco ldan
31a3(rgyud): 'das kyis rjesu yid rang ba mdzad te/rdo rje 'chang khyod song la lha rnaMs dang/lha'i bu rnaMs dang/lha ma yin rnaMs dang/lha ma yin gi bu rnaMs dang/klung lha rnaMs dang/klung lha'i bu rnaMs dang/namakha'
31a4(rgyud): lding rnaMs dang/namakha' lding gi bu rnaMs nas/'dus pa chen po'i bar dag sgra 'byin cing/nyaMs thag pa nyong ba rnaMs kyi bsaMs//pa rdzogs par gis shig/de nas rdo rje 'chang stan
31a5(rgyud): las langs te/lha dang lha ma yin rnams dang/klung lha dang/namakhA lding dang/'byung po'i tshogs gi nang du song nas/rjesu ston cing/rdo rje mtshon cha'i me sgron/me zhes bya ba'i ting
31a6(rgyud): nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs te/rdzu 'phrul gi stong gsuM gi stong chen po'i/'jig rten gi khaMsu/me lce 'bab ba'i 'od 'phro ba dang/me stag rnam par 'phro ba dang/lce 'bar ba'i klog rnaMs kyis
31a7(rgyud): 'jig rten gyi khaMs rnaMs gzir to//de nas skal pa chen po lngar rmi laM dang/chu dang gang ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi grangs dang tshad tsam gi 'dangs dang/gzi rjid dang/tshangs par spyod pa dang/gtsang
31a8(rgyud): ba dang;bzod pa dang/tshul khrims dang/rtul shugs dang/dka' thub dang/rang gi long spyod thos cad las byung ba/las grub pa'i rdo rje dang/byang chub seMs dpa' lag na rdo rje'i lag tu byin no//
31b1(rgyud): de ni sangs rgyas bco ldan 'das rnaMs kyis gsung rabs kyis chos ston par 'gyur ba/dgra bcoM pa rnaMs kyi chos dang/tshul khrims dang/ting nge 'dzin byed pa/byang chub seMs dpa' rnaMs kyi pha rol
31b2(rgyud): tu phyin pa dang/tshul pa khrims dang/bzod pa dang/snying rje byed pa rang gi rdo rje dang/rigs kyi rig pa dang/dkyil 'khor dang/legs pa'i tshul tu gyur pa dang/grag po dang dbang po dang/gshin
31b3(rgyud): rje dang/chu lha dang/lus dang/rlung lha dang/namakha' lding gtul dka' ba/rnaMs 'dul ba ste/de ltar de las byung ba'i bco ldan 'das/khro bo drag po rnaMs rmongs par byed/rgyu dang ldan pa

65a4(rgyud): bshad de//rang bzhin gyur cing chags la//rmi lam nang na dngos grub thob//de la yang ni dngos grub yod/rigs sngags dam tshig 'chad pa yi//bla ma la ston pa bzhin//

65a6(rgyud): blo ldan de ni dngos grub thob//sgrub pa po mtshan nyid rim par phye ba'o////de nas gzhan yang sgrub pa po'i//bya ba'i cho ga dag la yang//ji lta bu dag shes pa yi//sngags g.yogs dag gi
65a7(rgyud): mtshan nyid bshad//rigs btsun chos spyod dad pa can//sems stobs che la rtsol phod dpa'//bden par smra zhing gus pa che//yan lag ma nyes ma smad pa//ha cang mi ring mi sbom la//
65a8(rgyud): skyeg po ma yin mi thung min//ha cang kha dog gcig min pa'i//nad med sngags g.yog rab tu shes//bsdug bsngal thaMs cad bzod cing btan//las rnaMs kun la rab tu bzo'//bzod
65b1(rgyud): dang ldan zhing dka' thub can//rjesu mthun zhing dga' bar smra//blo na ldan zhing nga rgyal med//phan tshun 'gran pa dag kyang spang//mkhas shing byang la byas pa can//dran par ldan zhing rtong
65b2(rgyud): yang phod;gsang sngags rigs sngags dbye ba shes//gsang sngags bzlas shing shes rab rno//srung laswogs pa'i las rnaMs la//mkhas pas ('a)sngags pa'i g.yog du bzung//
65b3(rgyud): legs par sdams shing dam tshig shes/stan pa la ni dga' ba dang//bzlas brjod shes pa'i byed pa'i brda' shes pa//dam pa de ni grogasu 'dod//bas pa bzang zhing mi skyo
65b4(rgyud): la;ma grub bar du mi gtong zhing//sems stobs che ba'i grogs sten nas//bzlas pa po ni 'grub par 'gyur;;long spyod chen po dag la yang/gang zhig chags pa
65b5(rgyud): med pa dang//gus shing phan la spro ba can//de ni grogs por yongasu grags//dngos grub gi ni rdzas dag gis//gzhan kyi rab tu breng gyur kyang//chu gang gis ni mi gtong
65b6(rgyud): ba;;des ni grogs po nus par shes;;gang zhig sgrub pa'i bar du ni//dgos pa med pa sgrub pa la//byas par yang ni byed gyur pa//des ni g.yog po nus pa yin//yun ring du ni
65b7(rgyud): 'bras med dam//'tshe ba rnam pa sna tshogs kyis//gang zhig skye bar ma 'gyur ba//de ni gcig bu g.yog po yin//sgrub pa'i grogs mchog yon tan yang//de lta bur ni shes
65b8(rgyud): par bya;;khyad par du ni rgyud rnaMs dang//dkyil 'khor dag la de mkhas so//blo ldan gang zhig sgrub pa pos//yon tan gang gis lhag gyur pa//sgrub pa'i las kyis lhag ma rnaMs;
66a1(rgyud): @#//des ni gcig pus byed par nus//dbang skur cho ga sbyin sreg dang//dri la sogs pa gtsang ba dang//gang dag srung la sogs pa'i las//de dag blo ldan des bya'o//
66a2(rgyud): des ni phyi nang thaMs cad kyi//spyad par bya ba gces par yang//rgyud las gsungs pa'i cho ga yis//legs par rdzogs pa nyid du bya//sgrub pa'i las ni nar ma la//nyaMs par gyur pa
66a3(rgyud): yod na yang//de'i bya ba la dmigs nas//des ni de la 'jug par bya//rdzas kyi las rnaMs gzhan na yang//rang gis sbyin skyed bya ba 'am//rdzas la dran zhing ci gnas kyang//grub par
66a4(rgyud): bar chad 'byung mi bya//bzlos pa rtsa nga sgra (man)gis ni//bzlas brjod btang ba'i dus na yang//de yis de'i sngags /zlas pa//rtag par rab tu brtson par bya//grogs po
66a5(rgyud): yi ni sgo ba la//mi smra'i gtul shugs mi spyod do//de'i sdaMs pa yang de dag pas//de phyir rab tu 'jug par bya//de'i kha zas gang yin pa//de ni rjesu zos pas za//
66a6(rgyud): slob dpon mtshan nyid sgrub pa dang//bsngags g.yog rnaMs kyi mtshan nyid ni//gang dag gsungs pa de dag nyid//sgrub pa'i grogs mchog yon tan de'o//sngags g.yog gi mtshan nyid
